cāli, kali, tamiḻ eṉṟa mūṉṟuvakai āṇṭukaḷum tiṅkaḷ nāḷ kiḻamai ōraikaḷum pala paṭṭayaṅkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉārukku iṉṉār muṉpāka iṉṉār koṭutta tarmacācaṉappaṭṭayam eṉṟu eḻutumuṟaiyaik kāṇkiṉṟōm. viḷakkamākavum muṭivil tokuttum cācaṉa nōkkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. camayattuṟaiyil yār yārkku eṉṉeṉṉa mariyātaikaḷ, mariyātai varicaikaḷ avvaricaikkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ, aṟputa nikaḻccikaḷ, pūcai naṭappukkaḷ, annaṭappukku vēṇṭum poruḷ taraṅkaḷ ellām aiyattiṟkum vaḻuviṟkum iṭamiṉṟik kuṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟaikkuṟikkum nōkkamē ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya nōkkamākum. oru paḻaṅ kaiyeḻuttup puttakappaṭiyiliruntu innūl accākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum mūlappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ ēṭukaḷāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu aṟiyavēṇṭum.
ovvoru paṭṭayattiṟkum ettaṉai ēṭukaḷ eṉpataṉai mēl aṭṭavaṇaiyiṟ kāṇalām. ivvaṟap paṇiyai ulakam uḷḷavarai naṭattuvōm eṉavum, etu nilai tirintālum ivvaṟanilai tiriyōm eṉavum, ivvaṟattiṟku iṭaiyūṟu ceytavarkaḷ iṉṉapaḻikku āḷāvārkaḷ eṉavum, utavi ceytavarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam vāḻvārkaḷ eṉavum iṟutiyil viṭātu kuṟippiṭuvatu ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻakkāṟākum. iccācaṉam eḻutiṉatu iṉṉār coṟpaṭikku iṉṉāṉ eṉṟu kāṭṭi muṭikkappaṭum.  yār eḻutiṉāl cācaṉa nōkkam valuppaṭumō avaraikkoṇṭu eḻutaccollum aṟivuṭai maiyaip pārkkiṉṟōm. paṭṭayattiṉ toṭakkattilum kaṭaiciyilum murukaṉ pāṭalkaḷ iruttaluṇṭu. iṉi ovvoru paṭṭayattiṉ karuttiṉaiyum curukkamākak kāṇpōm.
1. paḻaṉikkōyil naṭappu aṭṭavaṇai (pakkam 1- 27)
nēmaṅkōyil iḷanala vakuppu kuppāpiccañ ceṭṭi makaṉ kumarappaṉ paḻaṉittalañ ceṉṟu uppu vaṇikañ ceytatu, paḻaṉikkōyil teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattu maṉaiyil taṅkip paṇṭārattiṉ utaviyaiyum avarmaṉaivi, pārvatiyammāḷ utaviyaiyum peṟṟatu. vēlāyuta cuvāmikku ilāpattil makamaip paṇam cērttatu, piṟakōyil nakarac ceṭṭiyārkaḷum kumarappaṉōṭu uppu vāṇikattiṟkuc cērntu koṇṭatu. uppuc ceṭṭikaḷ eṉṟa peyar peṟṟatu. (kalāmaṭam)īcāṉiya civācāriyār kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi kumarappaṉ paḻaṉikkuk kāvaṭi kaṭṭiyatu, iṉṉiṉṉa iṭattil taṅkic ceṉṟu,aintu nāḷ makēcuvara pūcai ceyvatu, ēḻu kōvil nakarattārum kāvaṭi eṭuttatu. īcāṉiya kurutēcikar kumarappaṉ mutalāṉavar paciyaip pōkkiyatu, pūcaikkup piramputtaṭi aruḷiyatu, kumarappaṉ campaṭṭiyil nattaliṅka nāyakkar maṉaiviyiṉ vayiṟṟuvaliyaip paḻaṉiyappar aruḷāṟ pōkkiyatu. nāyakkar maṉaivi kumarappaṉ vīṭu tēṭivantu naṉṟi celuttiyatu, nāyakkar iraṇṭu vēlāyutam paḻaṉiyapparukkuk koṭuttatu, vēlāyuta cuvāmi pārvatiyammāḷukkum kumarappaṉ peṇcāti ciṭṭāḷukkum īcāṉiyacivācāri yārukkum āyikkuṭi camīṉtārukkum kaṉavil tōṉṟic colliyatu, camīṉtāriṉ curanōyaip pōkkiyatu, maṇṭakappaṭi naṭattuvatu, paṇṭārattu maṉaiyai maṭamāka vaittuk koḷvatu, paṇṭārattukkuk kuṭumpattārkkum vaḻi vaḻi naṉṟi maṟavātiruppatu eṉṟa karuttukkaḷum iṉṉārukku iṉṉa koṭuppatu eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷai varaṉmuṟaikaḷum ivvaṭṭavaṇaiyil collappaṭṭuḷa.
maṇṭakappaṭiyil paṇṭārattukku aritip parivaṭṭamum, kumarappaṉukku alaṅkārap parivaṭṭamum, nakarattārkaḷil mutalāvatu tirunīṟum kāḷāñciyum koṭuppatu eṉavum, nakarappotuviliruntu (kalāmaṭam) īcāṉiya kurutēcikarukkum (pātarakkuṭi) tiruppuṉal tēcikarukkum, teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukkum, kumarappaṉukkum koṭuppatu eṉavum, iṉam oṉṟukkut tirunīṟṟuppai oṉṟu koṭuppatu eṉavum mariyātaikaḷ varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (pak.24)
2. ēḻunakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 28 -42)
itu teyvacikāmaṇi paṇṭārattu makaṉ teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum eḻutikkoṭutta tarumacācaṉam. cāli āṇṭu 1780 eṉpatu piḻaiyeṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. kaliyāṇṭōṭum piṟacācaṉaṅkaḷōṭum ottiṭṭup pārkkumpōtu, cāli 1520 eṉa iruttal vēṇṭum pōlum. yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ, nakarattār perumaikaḷ, ēḻu nakarattāriṉ irupattiraṇṭu vakuppukaḷ, teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukkum aṉṉatāṉa maṭattut tiruviḻāviṟkum koṭukkavēṇṭum arici mutalāṉa poruḷaḷavukaḷ, tīrttamum tirunīṟum vaḻaṅkum mariyātai varicaikaḷ, pattāntirunāḷ maṇṭakappaṭiyil koṭukkum poruḷaḷavukaḷ, taṅki varavēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷ ellām iccācaṉattu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cōḻavaracaṉ nakarattār peṇṇaimaruvip poṟukka muṭiyāta tontaravu ceytapaṭiyāl, iḷaiyāttaṅkuṭi vanta ceytiyaiyum, araca taṇṭaṉaikku okkūruṭaiyāṉ maṟutalai koṭuttapaṭiyāl nakarakkōyilkaḷil okkūruṭaiyāṉukku mutal mariyātai naṭakkiṉṟa ceytiyaiyum aṟikiṉṟōm; eṉiṉum, nēmam kumarappaṉ paḻaṉittalattukku uppu viṟkavantu paṭṭapirayācaiyai matittu, paḻaṉittalattil mutal vipūtiyum kāḷāñciyum parivaṭṭamum kumarappaṉukku naṭakkaṭṭum eṉṟu (pak.38) eḻu nakarattār aṉaivōrum orēmaṉatāyppēci muṭittaṉar eṉpatu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa karuttākum. iccācaṉam nakarattār coṟpaṭikku okkūruṭaiyāṉ uṭaiyappa ceṭṭi aṭaikkappaṉ eḻutiyatu. okkūruṭaiyāṉ taṉakku uriya mutal mariyātaiyaip paḻaṉittalattil nēmam kumarappaṉukku viṭṭukkoṭukka vēṇṭiyiruppatāl, iccācaṉattai okkūruṭaiyāṉaik koṇṭu eḻutac colliṉar nakarattār eṉa aṟiyavēṇṭum. ēḻu nakarattārum taṉittaṉi āṟu kāl kūṭattil kumarappaṉaik kumpiṭṭu viḻuntu tirunīṟu vāṅkikkoḷvatu eṉṟa mariyātaic ceytiyum, kumarappaṉ cantatiyil mutalvaruṭam paṭṭam kaṭṭukiṉṟa vivaramum aṟiyalām.
3. ēḻu nakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 42 - 65.)
itu cāli 1688 - kali 4868 (ki.pi.1766) ām āṇṭil kuḻantai paṇṭārattu makaṉ kumāracāmi paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum aḷitta cācaṉappaṭṭayam. mēlaiccācaṉam pōla yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum nakarattār perumaikaḷum pirivukaḷum paṇṭārattukkum kuṭumpa vaḻiyiṉarkkum koṭukkum kaṭṭaḷaikaḷum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇikkōyil pe. kuṭṭayaṉ vaikāvūr nāṭṭu vicayakiri vēlāyutat turaiyar kaḷukku āṇ kuḻantai piṟakkum eṉṟu colliyatu, turaiyiṉ paṭṭattammāḷ kuṭṭayaṉiṭam makappēṟu kēṭṭatu, kuṉṉak kuṭiyil periyamarutu pāṇṭiyar mūṉṟu kēḷvi kēṭṭut takka viṭaipeṟṟatu, kuṭṭayaṉukkuk kaṇaiyāḻiyum yāṉaik kompum koṭuttatu eṉṟa aṟputaṅkaḷai aṟiyalām.
vicayakirit turaiyum marutupāṇṭiyarum uttara viṭṭapaṭi, iraṇikkōyil pe. kuṭṭayaṉukku aruḷāṭum kāraṇattāl, iraṇṭāvatu kāḷāñciyum mariyātaik kāḷāñciyum koṭuppatu eṉṟa muṭivu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa ceytiyākum. mariyātai varicaikaḷum kāraṇaṅkaḷum muṟaikaḷum 59 - 62ām pakkam varai virivāka iṭam peṟṟuḷa. ippaṭṭayattil vicayanakarattu aracarkaḷaiyum avarkaḷ vaḻiyāṇṭa nāyakkar kaḷaiyum makāmaṇṭalēcuvaraṉ eḻupattēḻu pāḷaiyam mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum paṟṟic ciṟanta kuṟippukkaḷ uḷa (pak. 47 - 49)
4. ēḻu nakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 66 -78)
itu cāli 1710 kali 4889 (ki.pi. 1788) ām āṇṭil kumāracāmi paṇṭārattu makaṉ teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum koṭutta taruma cācaṉappaṭṭayam. nakarattārkaḷiṉ perumaikaḷ pirivukaḷ aintu nāḷ pūcaikkum aṉṉatāṉa maṭattut tiruviḻāvukkum koṭukkum arici poṉ paḻam mutaliyavai, paḷḷayattukkuk koṭukkum poruḷ aḷavukaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa. veṟṟilai pākku paḻam kāykaṟi kaḷōṭu pōyilaiyum curuṭṭum koṭuppatuṇṭu eṉṟa oru putucceytiyai ippaṭṭayattil kaṟkiṉṟōm. nēmaṅkōvil kumarappaṉukkum iraṇikkōyil kuṭṭayaṉukkum piṟarukkum uriya mariyātai varicaikaḷum kāraṇaṅkaḷum 73 -76 ām pakkaṅkaḷil iṭampeṟṟuḷa. teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattu maṉaiyil muṉpirunta āṟukāl kūraik kūṭattaik kaltiruppaṇi ceytu āṟukāl cavukkaiyākkiyatum, 78 kāl tiruppaṇi maṇṭapam ceytatum kuṟikkattakka muṉṉēṟṟaṅ kaḷām. iṅṅaṉam nakarattār celavu ceytu paṇi perukki ṉālum aṉupavikkum urimai paṇṭārattu vaḻiyiṉarkku eṉṟum uṇṭu. vayiravaṉ kōyil vēlappa ceṭṭi vayiravaṉ tiruppaṇi vēlaiyil mikkamuyaṟci eṭuttuk koṇṭataṉālum, cuvāmi vayiravaṉiṭam nēril tōṉṟip pēciyataṉālum (pak.76) nakarattārkaḷil mūṉṟāvatu mariyātai vayiravaṉukku uriyatu eṉpatu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa ceytiyākum.
5. piṟāmalai ātīṉam naṭappu aṭṭavaṇai (pakkam 79 -85)
paḻaṉikkōyil iṭumpaṉmalai mutal ūr varum varaiyil īcāṉiya civācāriyār, paṇṭāram, kumarappaṉ nakarattār ceyyavēṇṭiya muṟaikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. iṉṉārukku iṉṉa kaṭamai eṉṟu caṉṉitāṉam colliyapōtu ātīṉa mariyātai taṉakkum vēṇṭum eṉṟu iraṇikkōyil tiruvēṭpū ruṭaiyāṉ kuṭṭayaṉ kēṭṭāṉ. maṟṟavarkaḷaik kalantālōcittu, tiruvāviṉaṉkuṭiyil naṭakkiṟa paṭṭuparivaṭṭamum antac celavum kuṭṭayaṉ taṉatāka irukkaṭṭum eṉṟu cuvāmi colliyatu. caṉṉitāṉam ūrukku varumpōtu kuṭṭayaṉukku mūṉṟāvatu paṭṭup pītāmparam koṭukkiṉṟatu.
6. ātīṉam paṭṭāpiṭēka aṭṭavaṇai (pakkam 86 -90)
kumarappaṉ paḻaṉiyāṇṭavar aṉukkirakam peṟṟa paṭiyāṉum, ātīṉattukku vēṇṭum kāriyaṅkaḷum uḻaippum ceytapaṭiyālum ātīṉappaṭṭāpiṭēkattu. iraṇṭāvatākak kumarappaṉ paṭṭu cāttukiṉṟatu eṉṟu īcāṉakurutēcikar moḻintār. itaṉai irupattoru kōttirattu nakarattār kaḷum cariyeṉa ottukkoṇṭaṉar. vaḻakkampōl naṭakka vēṇumēyoḻiyap putuvaḻakkam ceyyappaṭātu eṉṟu iḷaiyāttāṅkuṭi paṭṭaṇacuvāmi vakuppu aruṇācalañ ceṭṭi aṇṇāmalai maṭṭum caṉṉitāṉattiṉ tiruvaṭiyil viḻuntu maṟuppuk kūṟiṉār. kumarappaṉ perumaiyaik kurutēcikar eṭutturaittapiṉ aṇṇāmalaiyum ottuk koṇṭār. ottukkoṇṭataṟku uṟutiyāka aṇṇā malaiyaiyē aṭṭavaṇai eḻutumpaṭi īcāṉiya civācāriyār kaṇittār.
7. maṭavālayat tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 91 - 100)
itu cāli 1727 kali 4906 (ki.pi.1805) āṇṭil nākappa paṇṭārattu makaṉ kuḻantaivēlup paṇṭārattukku ēḻunakarattār aṉaivōrum koṭutta cācaṉappaṭṭaya mākum. muṉṉaiccācaṉaṅkaḷ pōlap paṇṭārattukkuk koṭukkum naṭappukkaḷum piṟavum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa. iṭam pōtāmaiyiṉāl aṭuttamaṉai mōḷaiyattēvaṉ vakaiyaṟā iṭattai vilaikku vāṅki ākkupuraiyum muppattoṉpatu kāl tiruppaṇi maṇṭapamum kaṭṭiya vaḷarcci kuṟippiṭattakum. maṭattiṟkāka vāṅkiya immaṉaikaḷum paṇṭārattiṉ anupōkattiṟku uriyaṉavē piḷḷaiyārkōyil vakuppu muttappaṉukkuc civappaṭṭiyil paḻaṉiyāṇṭavaruṭaiya aruḷvanta ceytiyum, toṇṭaimāṉ aracar muttappaṉ vīṭṭiṟku vantu niṉaitta kāriyattaik kēṭṭu makiḻntu cālvai mutaliyaṉa koṭutta ceytiyum, muttappaṉ kēṭṭuk koṇṭapaṭi kōvil vīṭṭukkut tāmpūravēl koṭutta ceytiyum, nēmaṅkōvil paḻaṉiyappaṉ paḻaṉikkōvilukkuk kāvaṭi eṭuttu varumpōtu inta vēlāyutattaiyum vaittuppūcai ceytuvarumpaṭi colliya ceytiyum, itaṉaic colvataṟkākap paḻaṉiyappaṉai araṇmaṉaikku aḻaittuvantu mariyātai ceyta ceytiyum ippaṭṭayattukku mukkiya ceytikaḷākum. (pak. 96 -98) tēvakōṭṭai rattiṉavēlāyutamum kuṉṟakkuṭi vēlāyutamum cērttukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
8. nirampavaḻakiya tēcikar paṭṭayam (pakkam 100 -116)
itu cāli 1549 kali 4730 (ki.pi.1627) ām āṇṭil ēḻu nakarattār muṉpākak kuṉṟakkuṭi ātīṉam nirampa vaḻaṅkiya tēcikar (tuḷāvūr maṭam) koṭutta paṭṭayam. yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyar kaḷum nāyakkar peyarkaḷum muṉṉark kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tēcikar perumai, nakarattār perumai, kōttiram, kulacēkarapura cavuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ nakarattārkaḷai naṟkuṭiyākat taṉ cīmaikku aḻaittatu, nakarattāriṉ veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷ cōḻaṉ uttira viṭṭum (kalāmaṭam) īcāṉiya kurutēcikarai toḻa maṟuttatu eṅkaḷ nirampavaḻakiya kurutēcikar (tuḷāmaṭam) pāṇṭināṭṭiṟku vantāl nāṅkaḷum pōvōm eṉṟu uraittatu, veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷ kuruviṭam muṟaiyiṭṭatu, cōḻaṉ kurutēcikarai varavaḻaittup pāṇṭiyaṉ pala kirāmaṅkaḷ taruvāṉ eṉṟu moḻintu irupattiraṇṭu kōttirattu nakarattār āṇkaḷum veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷum pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟku vantatu, pāṇṭiyaṉ ellōraiyum etir koṇṭaḻaittu mākāṇaṅkaḷ carvamāṉiyamāka aḷittatu. kuṉṟakkuṭi ātīṉam amaintatu mutalāṉa nakarattār varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷai ippaṭṭayattut teḷivāka aṟiyalām.
nēmaṅkōyil kumarappaṉum avaṉ maṉaivi veḷḷāṭṭi ciṭṭāḷum uppumāṟi eṭutta makamaiyāl inta ātīṉamum piṟa tarumaṅkaḷum naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉavum, paḻaṉittalattilpōla intattalattilum nakarattārkaḷil mutalāvatu tīrttam tirunīṟu kumarappaṉukkuk koṭuppatu eṉavum nirampavaḻakiya tēcikar uttaravuppaṭi nakarattār aṉaivōrum uṭaṉpaṭṭa ceytiyai ippaṭṭayam mukkiyamākac collukiṉṟatu. mariyātai varicaikaḷ 114 -15 ām pakkaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa.
paṭṭayaṅkaḷum naṭaimuṟaikaḷum
paṭṭayaṅkaḷil kuṟittuḷ naṭappukkaḷ iṉṟum perumpālum mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. kālappōkkil cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkakkūṭum. ellāp paṭṭayaṅkaḷilum mutal mariyātaikkuriyavarāṉa nēmam kumarappaṉ vaḻi vantōrē iṉṟu neṟkuppai ku.paḻa. vīṭṭiṉarāvar. iraṇṭām mariyātaikku uriya aruḷāṭi iraṇikkōyil pe.kuṭṭayaṉ vaḻivantōrē iṉṟu kaṇṭaṉūr kupa.cu. vīṭṭiṉar nakarattārkaḷil mūṉṟām mariyātaikku uriya vayiravaṉkōyil vayiravaṉ vaḻivantōrē iṉṟu kāraikkuṭi kuma.peri.cu.paḻa. vīṭṭiṉar. tāmpūravēl peṟṟa piḷḷaiyōr kōvil muttappaṉ vaḻivantavarē iṉṟu mēlaiccivapuri āṟu.muru. kuṭumpattiṉar iṅṅaṉam teyva aruḷpeṟṟa nakarattār nālvar vaḻivantōr ālpōl taḻaittu vāḻntu varutaliṉ, muṉṉōr paṇikaḷ iṭaiyaṟātu kākkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa uṇartal vēṇṭum. ikkuṭumpattārkaḷ tam kuḻantaikaḷukku murukaṉ peyarkaḷaiyē vaippar eṉpatum niṉaiyattakum.
paṭṭayaṅkaḷum tamiḻum
ēṟakkuṟaiya munnūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉtōṉṟiya iccācaṉat tamiḻ palavakaiyālum ūṉṟi ārāyvataṟku uriyatu eṉṟu maṭṭum īṇṭuc cuṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ. tamiḻ urainaṭai varalāṟṟil iccācaṉa naṭai iṭampeṟat takuvatu. oru perum puttaka aḷaviṟku oppāṉa iccācaṉap paṭṭayaṅkaḷ pēcuvatu pōṉṟa tamiḻ naṭaiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷa. oṇṇu, reṇṭu, mūṇu, paṉireṇṭu, aruci, kuṭuttu, rācā, pōyi, tuṇṇuttuppai, matiyāri, ciṟaicākkiṉai, vīṭṭukkup pōratu, pokkucam, naccēttiram eṉappala marūuccoṟkaḷai nirampakkāṇalām. akkālattut tamiḻccoṟkaḷai eṅṅaṉam olittārkaḷ eṉṟum aṟintu koḷḷalām. māṟi (viṟṟu) atumēraikku (atupōla) paraṅkaḷ (paratēcikaḷ) vaḷamai (vaḻakkam) peṇṇāṭṭi (peṇṭāṭṭi) neṭṭeḻuttu (kaiyeḻuttu) eṉṟa coṟkaḷaiyum, aṭakeṭuttal, aritipparivaṭṭam, alaṅkārap parivaṭṭam, vēṟpaṭi, aruḷāṭi mutalāṉa coṟkaḷaiyum aṟikiṉṟōm. uṇṭumā, meḻukivaittuvai. nāṉum varuka, varuvōmākavum eṉṟa toṭar muṭipukaḷaiyum aṟikiṉṟōm.
nakarattārkaḷ oḷavaiyiṉ tamiḻppāṭṭukkup poṟpāṭakam koṭuttārkaḷ eṉavum, varappuyara eṉṟu oḷavai uḻavarkaḷai vāḻttiyatu pōla maṭiccīlai peruka eṉṟu vaṇikañceyyum innakarattārkaḷai vāḻtti varaṅkoṭuttār eṉavum ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ viḷampukiṉṟaṉa. nāyakka maṉṉaṉ muttamiḻ aṇaṅkaṉ eṉavum, vaḷḷuvamaṟaiyuṅ kaṟṟu muppāl moḻiyumpaṭiyē aṟivai aṟintu allavai kaṭintu nallavai nāṭṭik kuṭitaḻaikka āṇṭāṉ eṉavum moḻikiṉṟaṉa. nāṅkaḷ ēḻu nakarattārkaḷ naṭṭakallaiyum iṭṭacūlamum aḻikka māṭṭōm; makāmērukiri cāyntapōtilum, kaṭal ēḻum vaṟṟiṉa pōtilum, kāvēri vaṟaṇṭa pōtilum, cantirāḷ cūriyāḷ teṟku vaṭakkāṉapōtilum nāṅkaḷ nakaram poycolla māṭṭōm eṉavarum uṟutippāṭukaḷ nakarattāriṉ nāṇayattaip paṟaicāṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ūḻi peyariṉum tām peyarār cāṉṟāṇmaikku
āḻi yeṉappaṭu vār
eṉṟa vaḷḷuvamaṟaippaṭi vāḻntavarkaḷ nakarattārkaḷ eṉpataṉai inta aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ kāṭṭikkoṇṭu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭaiyōr tam poyyāp paṇpiṉaiyum kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉaiyum pukaḻiṉaiyum oḻuṅkiṉaiyum aṟitaṟku ippaṭṭayanūl ārāycciyāḷar kaippaṭṭupparavum eṉṟu etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ. nakarattār camutāyam tamiḻpperuñ camutāyattiṉ oru pakutiyātaliṉ, ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ tamiḻiṉattiṉ nākarika vaḷarcciyaik kāṇa muyalvārkkut tuṇaiceyyum eṉṟu cuṭṭikkāṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ.
peṟutaṟkariya tammuṉṉōriṉ tarumap paṭṭayaṅkaḷai yāvarum aṟiyum vaṇṇam accu nūlvaṭivākki utaviya tiru.ku.paḻa.cu.paḻa.cuppiramaṇiya ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷukkut tamiḻulakam mikka kaṭappāṭu uṭaiyatu. ellā nakarattār kaḷuṭaiya poruḷutaviyiṉālum ivveḷiyīṭu varukiṉṟatu. nakarattārkaḷukku ellāt tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ naṉṟiyuriyatu.
ṭākṭar. va.cupa.māṇikkam


nītinūlkaḷ
 māṇikkaṉār vakutta eḷiya urai
mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār uḷḷam, col, ceyalkaḷāl tūyar. nāṭu nalam peṟa naṟkaruttukkaḷai nāḷum eṇṇi eḻutiyavar. ciṟuvar neñcakaḷil naṟkaruttukkaḷ nāḷum vitaikkap peṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟa nalla nōkkiṉar. ātti cūṭi amaippil ‘tamiḻccūṭi’ iyaṟṟic ciṟuvar uḷḷamum vāḻvum ceḻikka vaḻivakai kaṇṭavar. nalla karuttukkaḷaip parappum nōkkil ‘māṇikkak kuṟaḷ’ iyaṟṟiya māṇpiṉar. tirukkuṟaḷ cintaṉaikaḷait teḷivāka uṇartta cevviya urai kaṇṭa cemmal.
kāraikkuṭiyil tamiḻccaṅkam amaittu ataṉ talaivarāy viḷaṅkiya māṇikkaṉār tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷaiyum nīti nūlkaḷaiyum ciṟuvarkaḷ neñcakattil eṉṟum nilai niṟutta virumpiṉār. ātticūṭi, koṉṟai vēntaṉ, naṟuntokai, mūturai, nalvaḻi, naṉṉeṟi, ulaka nīti ākiya nūlkaḷukku eḷiya teḷivurai vakuttut tamiḻc caṅka veḷiyīṭākap pala patippukkaḷ varac ceytār.  tamiḻccaṅkam mūlam nīti nūl oppuvittal pōṭṭi naṭatti āṇṭutōṟum ciṟuvarkaḷukkut toṭarntu paricukaḷ nalkiṉār. vaḷarum payirukku uramum nīrum oḷiyum aḷittuk kāppatil vallavar. ceṟivu naṭaiyum pulamai naṭaiyum valla māṇikkaṉār ciṟupar poruṭṭu mika mika ḷayi tamiḻil paḻaku naṭaiyil urai vakuttuḷḷār.
nīti nūl karuttukkaḷ parava vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇattiṟkuc ceyal vaṭivam koṭuttār. kāraikkuṭi tamiḻc caṅkattaip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭār. payiṟṟu vittār. paricukaḷ nalkiṉār. nīti nūlkaḷukku eḷiya urai vakuttār.
ceyal ceyal eṉa vāḻnta cemmal. vāyccol vīrarāka vāḻāmal colliyavaṇṇam ceytu muṭitta perumakaṉārākavum tikaḻntār.
ivar, kaṟṟal, kaṟpittal mutaliya kalvi neṟikaḷil cīriya kōṭpāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vāḻnta pērāciriyar. ivaratu eḻuttum pēccum ivartam kalvik koḷkaikaḷaip paṟaicāṟṟu kiṉṟaṉa. tūya vāḻviḷarākiya mūtaṟiñar naṉmuṟaiyil nīti nūl iyaṟṟattiṭṭamiṭṭiruntār. ivaratu vāḻkkaik kuṟippu itaṉai uṇarttukiṟatu. ciṟuvarkaḷukkuriya nīti nūlkaḷaip paṟṟi pērāciriyar eḻutiya āyvuk kaṭṭurai peruñ ciṟappuṭaiyatu.
iḷaiñarkaḷukku uṭal, uḷḷam, uyir paṟṟiya nalvaḻik karuttukkaḷ, nalvāḻvuk kōṭpāṭukaḷ tamiḻil evvāṟu collappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpatai nīḷa niṉaintavar. naṉku ārāyntavar. tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkōṭu tamiḻ aṟakkōṭ pāṭukaḷum vaḷarum kuḻantaikaḷ neñcil patiya vaippatil ceyal ārvam kāṭṭiyavar.
	ātticēr koṉṟai aḻakutamiḻ mūturai
	pātticēr nalvaḻi paṇpulakam - pūtta
	naṟuntokai naṉṉeṟi ēḻum kuḻantaik
	kuṟuntamiḻ eṉṟaṟintu koḷ.
nītinūlkaḷ paṟṟi māṇikkaṉār uḷḷam uvantu iyaṟṟiya ippāṭalai eṉṟum neñcil niṟuttuvōm.
tamiḻar vāḻvunalak kōṭpāṭukaḷai ēḻu nītinūlkaḷum ilakkiya iṉpamuṟum vakaiyil eṭuttiyampum tiṟattiṉai ivartam ārāycci muṉṉuraiyil aṟintu koḷḷalām. pērāciriyariṉ kaṉavu naṉavākat tamiḻnāṭṭut toṭakkap paḷḷitōṟum mīṇṭum nītinūl kalvi kaṭṭāyappaṭuttappaṭa vēṇṭum. inta ariya nalla paṇiyaic ceyta pērāciriyarkkut tamiḻ ulakam kaṭamaippaṭṭuḷḷatu. nītinūlkaḷaip pala patippukkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭup parappiya kāraikkuṭi tamiḻccaṅkac ceyalāḷar mā.a. ñāṉa cēkarapāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷukkum va.cupa.mā. eḻuttukkaḷ aṉaittum accil kāṇa avāvi niṟkum aṇṇal māṇikkaṉāriṉ iḷaval va.cupa.cokkaliṅkam avarkaḷukkum māṇikkaṉāriṉ aṉaittu nūlkaḷiṉ patippuc cemmaikku uṟutuṇaiyāy irukkum vāḻum va.cupa.māvākiya ṭākṭar irā.cāraṅkapāṇi avarkaḷukkum eṅkaḷ neñcārnta naṉṟi.
māṇikkaṉār eḻuttukkaḷ aṉaittaiyum ‘māṇikka nūl varicai’yil veḷiyiṭṭu makiḻntōm. inta āṇṭu tirukkuṟaḷukku māṇikka uraiyiṉaip patippittup pēruvakai koṇṭōm. toṭarntu māṇikkak kuṟaḷai veḷiyiṭṭu maṭṭilā makiḻcciyuṟṟōm. māṇikkaṉār iḷaiñar kaḷukkākavē eḷiya muṟaiyil eḻutiya nītinūlkaḷiṉ uraikaḷaip patippittup pēruvakai koḷkiṟōm.


The Tamil Concept of Love
 Preface
I deem it my sincere duty to present before the scholars of the world the noble principles of Aham Literature whose origin is as old as Tamil language itself and whose influence on all kinds of Tamil literature of all periods is incalculable. An elementary knowledge of Aham is indeed essential even for a beginner in Tamil. Without its study, Tamil culture and civilisation will be a sealed book.
Love is no doubt the common theme of any literature in any language. The selective nature of the love-aspects, the impersonal and algebraic form of the characters and the universal and practical treatment of the subject are the differentiating points of Ahattinai.
What is the impulse behind the creation of Aham literature? The unity of the family is the bed-rock of the unity of the world. The achievement of that conjugal unity depends upon the satisfaction of the sexual congress between the rightful lovers in youthhood. Dissatisfaction unconsciously disintegrates the family. There will be few problems in society, religion and politics, if family life is a  contented one and the husband and wife pay high regard to each other’s sexual hunger. Therefore sex education is imperative to every young man and woman before and after marriage.
How to educate them? The ancient Tamils saw in literature an effective and innocent means for instructing boys and girls in sexual principles and sexual experiences and with that noble motive created a well-defined literature called Ahattinai with inviolable rules. I hope the study of this book will be highly useful for promoting good relations between young lovers, whatever their race and nationality may be.
I am greatful to the authorities of the University of Madras for their kind permission to publish my thesis (“Love in Sangam Poetry”) submitted by me for the Ph.D. Degree in 1956.
My thanks are due to my colleagues Lieut. U. Bala Subramaniam M.A., and Thiru R. Sarangapani M.A., M. Litt. for their assistance in publishing this book.
I shall always cherish the selfless service of the Saiva Siddhanta Works Publishing Society for coming forward to publish my work.
‘KATHIRAKAM’			     						V. Sp. MANICKAM,
Karaikudi-2
15-5-1962
Collected Papers
Preface
The research papers presented and key-note addresses delivered in seminars and conferences and articles contributed to journals and souvenirs by me on different occassions are now being collected and published in the form of a book entitled collected papers. It is natural for any author to enjoy a delightful feeling when the stray flowers of his papers are strung together into a garland of book.
These seventeen papers are related to the varied aspects of Tamil Language. litereature, history and culture. It is gratifying to note that Tolkappiyam, the earliest Tamil work finds a place in many papers.
The author will feel rewarded if the discerning readers find that his views regarding the orderly and continued growth of Tamil are worthy of consideration and discussion.
My thanks are due to Thiru. C. Kathirvelu, Research Scholar, Annamalai University for going through the proof and M/s. Sivakami Printers for the neat printing and execution.
			     V. Sp. MANICKAM
	T.D.A.R. Centre, Karaikudi-2
15-6-1970

A  study Of Tamil Verbs
 Preface
It really gives me immense pleasure to publish after a long spell of twenty four years, my research thesis “Tamil Verbs” approved by the University of Madras for the award of M.O.L. Degree in 1948. I  am aware that most of the philological terms used in this thesis are not now in vogue and that new linguistic terms have replaced them. Notwithstanding the change of terminology, I do hope that the collection of material bearing on the several aspects of verbs in Tmail and the conclusions based on them are still valid in the field of linguistics. This prompted me to make this thesis available to researchers in print.
I feel honoured by the favour shown to me by the Vice-Chancellor and members of the Syndicate of the Annamalai University for sanctioning publication of this book in the University series. I am very grateful to them for their kindness.
It is my duty to record my sincere obligation to the late Dr. A. Chidambaranathan Chettiar, former Professor of Tamil in this University for his fruitful guidance in this research work of mine.
I wish to express my thanks to Dr. S. Agesthialingom, Director of the Centre of Advanced Study in Linguistics, Annamalai University for his valuable Foreword. My thanks are due to Dr. S.V. Shanmugam, Reader in Linguistics for going through the thesis before publication and for his useful suggestions and to Thiru K. Narayanan, M.A., Lecturer in Tamil for preparing Index.
V.Sp. Manickam
Annamalainagar, 12-10-72



tirumuṟai tillaiyampalam ēṟiyatu!
tēvāra tiruvācaka mutalāṉa teyvat tirumuṟaikaḷait tillai naṭarācar kōyilil kīḻē niṉṟutāṉ pāṭum vaḻakkam ituvarai iruntu vantatu. tirumuṟaikaḷai ampalattiṉ mēl pāṭa kurukkaḷ aṉumatippatillai. itu tirumuṟaikku matippuk kuṟaivāka iruntatu. maturaip palkalaik kaḻaka muṉṉāḷ tuṇaivēntar. va.cupa.māṇikkaṉār talaimaiyil tirumuṟai iyakkakkuḻu pōrāṭat toṭaṅkiyatu. tirumuṟaikaḷ kuṭamuḻukku viḻāviṟkuḷ tillaiyampalam ēṟāviṭṭāl uṇṇā nōṉpu toṭaṅkavum muṭivu ceytatu. makkaḷum, māṇavarkaḷum tiraḷāka itaṟku ātaravu tantaṉar. tamiḻaka aracum ikkōrikkaikku ottuḻaittatu.
kōṭṭāṭci uyar aluvalarkaḷ potuk kuḻu kurukkaḷiṭam tamiḻukkum tirumuṟaikkum uriya matippuk koṭukka vēṇṭum eṉavum 11.2.87 ām nāḷ naṭaipeṟum kuṭamuḻukku iṭaiyūṟiṉṟi naṭaipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉavum eṭuttuc coṉṉārkaḷ. uṇmai nilaiyai uṇarnta kurukkaḷ perumakkaḷ icaintaṉar. 1.2.87 ām nāḷ mutal tirumuṟaikaḷ tillaiyampalattiṉ mēl kurukkaḷāl pāṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. itu varalāṟṟuc ciṟappu mikka māṟṟamākum. itu pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa makkaṭku makiḻcciyaiyum uṇarcciyaiyum tantatu.
teyvat tamiḻt tirumuṟai talaimaiyāṉa tillaik kōyilil ampalam ēṟi muḻaṅkat toṭaṅki viṭṭatu. iṉi ellāk kōyilkaḷum tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭait tāṉē viraivil mēṟkoḷḷum eṉavum itaṟku ōr iyakkam tēvaippaṭātu eṉavum nampukiṉṟōm. makkaḷiṉ tamiḻp pakkuvattaiyum araciṉ tamiḻuṟutiyaiyum kōyilāḷarkaḷ purintu ceyaṟpaṭuvārkaḷāka
- teṉmoḻiyil eḻutiyatu, 
va.cupa. māṇikkam, kāraikkuṭi-2
talaivar, tirumuṟai iyakkakkuḻu.
tamiḻiṉ poṟkālam
irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muntiyē, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutikkālattē, tamiḻukkup poṟkālam toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu eṉṟuteḷikiṉṟēṉ. āṉanta cutantiram aṭaintu viṭṭōm eṉṟu āṭuvōmē paḷḷup pāṭuvōmē’ eṉap pāṭṭukkoru pulavaṉ pārati teḷintu,iṟanta kāla naṭaiyiṟ pāṭiṉāṉ; atu poykkavillai. nāṭuviṭutalai peṟṟa varalāṟu aṟivōm tolkāppiya marapiṉpaṭi etirkālattut teḷivum nampikkaiyum koṇṭa meymmaikaḷai iṟanta kālattuk kuṟippoṭu kiḷattalē tamiḻ naṭaiyākum. innaṭaippaṭi ñālam aḷanta tol putut  tamiḻ moḻi maḻalaip paḷḷi mutal palkalaik kaḻakam varai orē payiṟṟumoḻiyāka ākiviṭṭatu eṉavum kalaikkalvi, aṟiviyal kalvi, toḻiliyaṟ kalvi ellāttuṟaiyilum tamiḻ oṉṟē tamiḻakattil payiṟṟu moḻiyāyiṟṟu eṉavum tamiḻvaḻik kaṟṟavarkkē uyarkalvic cērkkai uṇṭu eṉavum tamiḻvaḻip payiṉṟavarkkē tamiḻakattil ellā vēlai vāyppukaḷum uṇṭu eṉavum naṉavu kāṇpōmāka!
maiyap pārāḷ maṉṟaṅkaḷil tamiḻum āṭci moḻiyākum urimai vāyppup peṟum eṉavum maiyavaracōṭu tamiḻilum eḻuttuṟavu naṭaipeṟum eṉavum ayalakat tūtaraṅkaḷilum tamiḻ nalliṭampeṟum eṉavum irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilēyē cīriḷamait tiṟam vāynta centamiḻiṉa kural aikkiya nāṭṭavaiyiṉ araṅkil muḻaṅkum eṉavum nampuvōmāka. innampikkai vēṇavāvāl collappaṭuvataṉṟu; tamiḻ itaṟkellām itaṟku mēlākavum takutip pēṟuṭaiya celva moḻi eṉpataṉai ulakam aṟiyum. ulaka moḻikaḷōṭu tamiḻiṉ varalāṟṟai oppiṭuvār tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ uḷḷāṟṟalai naṉkaṟivar. toṉmaiyil kuṟaivā? amaippil kuṟaivā? ilakkaṇa varampil kuṟaivā? nūṟcelvattil kuṟaivā? olit tūymaiyil kuṟaivā? nam tāymoḻiyiṉ mēṉmaiyai aṟiyakillāta tamiḻp pāvikaḷiṉ kuṟaivoṉṟait tavira tamiḻukku eṉṉa kuṟaivuṇṭu? niṟaivaik kuṟaiveṉṟu tūṟṟum maṭavōrkaḷiṉ aṭimaiyait tavirat tamiḻiṉ cemmaikku eṉṉa kuṟaipāṭu uṇṭu? ikkuṟai kūṟuvār ellām irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭil uṇmaiyai uṇarntu aṟivu cemmaippaṭṭu urimai pūṇṭu tamiḻk kaṭamai ceyvar eṉṟu nampuvōmāka! yāmaṟinta moḻikaḷilē tamiḻ moḻi pōl iṉitāvatu eṅkum kāṇōm eṉṟu kavippaṟaiyaṟainta pārati vākku kuṟaḷ vākkaṉṟō? eṅkum kāṇōm eṉpateṉṉa eṉṟum kāṇōm eṉṟa nilaikkut tamiḻ iṉṉum cila āṇṭukaḷilē uyarvaḷarcci peṟum. takutiyuṭaiya vallāḷaṉai yārum aṭakki vaikka muṭiyātatupōlat takuti mikka tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ pūtavāṟṟalai āṅkila mōkaṅkoṇṭa ettamiḻk kūṭṭamum taṭuttuviṭa muṭiyātu. muṭiyavē muṭiyātu. `tamiḻ eṅkaḷ uyirukku nēr’ eṉṟa moḻi vīrarkaḷ āṅkilap pācamum vācamum paṟṟiya evvaḷavu tāy tantaiyar taṭuttālum, tamiḻmoḻiyait tamiḻakattil ellāt tuṟaiyilum muṭimoḻiyākki veṟṟi kāṇpar; poruḷātāra vāḻvellām tamiḻvaḻikkē; piḻaippu vāypellām tamiḻvaḻikkē; koḷviṉai koṭuppiṉaiyellām tamiḻ vaḻikkē eṉṟu tamiḻmoḻi maṟavarkaḷ iṉṉum cilavāṇṭil moḻi vākai cūṭuvar. aṉṉait tamiḻē vēlai peṟu; āṅkilamē veḷiyēṟu eṉṟa iṭikkural muḻaṅkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. ittamiḻk kuṟikkōḷil nām ellām oṉṟātal kaṇṭu, eṅkum tamiḻ etilum tamiḻellā vēlaiyum tamiḻukkē eṉṟa putukkural kēṭṭu āṅkila mōka māyaikoṇṭa pakaivar iṉṉum cilavāṇṭiliruppiṭam teriyātu eṅkō maṟaintu viṭuvar; palar `tirunti viṭuvar. tamiḻ vāḻum vaḷarum kalvi uṟuppeṟum; ellāruma kalviccelvam peṟuvar; pārata nāṭutamiḻ vaḷarcciyāl uyarvu peṟum. itaṉālaṉṟō tēciyam pāṭiya pāratiyār vāḻiya centamiḻ vāḻka naṟṟamiḻar; vāḻiya pārata maṇit tirunāṭu’ eṉṟu kāraṇa kāriya muṟaippaṭi pāṭiṉār. ippāṭṭiṉ uṇmai eṉṉa teriyumā? pāratat tirunāṭṭiṉ vāḻvu tāṉum centamiḻiṉ vāḻvaic cārntirukkiṉṟatu; tamiḻ vāḻvu kuṉṟiṉāl pāratattiṉ tiruvumkuṉṟiviṭum eṉṟa kuṟippup pulappaṭavillaiyā? pāratiyai uṇarātavar pāratattaiyē uṇarātavar āvar.
eṅkum tamiḻ entuṟaiyum tamiḻ evvēlaiyum tamiḻukkē eṉṟu nām collumpōtu, iru karuttukkaḷait teḷiya vēṇṭum intiyāvil uḷḷa teluṅku, pañcāpi, kucarattu, inti mutaliya ēṉaimoḻikaḷ tattam iṭaṅkaḷil muḻuvurimai peṟṟu vaḷara vēṇṭuma eṉpatuvē namkoḷkai, iṉṟu āṅkila ātikkam intiyāviṉ enta moḻikaḷaiyum kālattukkēṟṟa uyarmoḻiyākavum vēlai vāyppu moḻiyākavum vaḷaraviṭavillai intiyarkaḷai vaḷarkkavum intiya moḻikaḷai vaḷarkkavumtāṉ intiyā urimai peṟṟatu. intiyarkaḷ āṅkilattai vaḷarkkavum āṅkilattāl oru cila intiyarkaḷ vaḷaravum nām viṭutalai peṟavillai. viṭutalaik kaṭamai eṉṉa eṉpatai nām uṇarntāka vēṇṭum. perumakkaḷ evvaḷavutāṉ poruḷātārattil vaḷarvatu pōlat tōṉṟiṉālum, makkaḷ moḻi urimaiyum vaḷarcciyum vēlaivāyppum, peṟātavarai, cintaṉaiyaṭimaiyākattāṉ irukkum urimaipeṟṟu nāṟpatu āṇṭukaḷākiyum nām iṉṉum aṭimaic cintaṉaiyāḷarkaḷākat tāṉ uyir vaittuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟōm. namvaḻiyiṉaraiyum āṅkila aṭimait taṉattiṟ paḻakkik koṇṭirukkiṟōm. namnāṭṭil uyarkalvi kaṟṟavarkaḷ ellām maṉattālum naṭaimuṟaiyālum ilaṇṭaṉaiyum amerikkāvaiyumtāṉ tāy nāṭākak karutukiṉṟārkaḷ. aṅkuk kuṭiyurimai peṟuvataittāṉ virumpukiṉṟārkaḷ. āṅkila mōkam āṅkila nāṭukaḷiṉ mōkamāki intiyat tēciyap pakaiyākiviṭṭatu. veḷippaṭṭa puṟappakaiyai viṭa āṅkila mōkaṅkoṇṭa uṭpakaiyē intiyat tēciyattiṟku aṇukkuṇṭākum. āṅkila mōkam aḻiyum nāḷ ennāḷō annāḷ intiyattēciyam vāḻum nāḷākum. ātaliṉ irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiya moḻikaḷ ellām maḻalai mutal palkalai varai uriya iṭaṅkaḷil payiṟṟu moḻikaḷ ākum eṉavum ellāvēlai vāyppum intiya moḻi vaḻiyiṉarkkē kiṭaikkum eṉavum naṉavu koḷvōmāka!
iṉṟaiya ulakac cūḻalil piṟa moḻiyum ayal moḻiyum kaṟpatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatu eṉṟa karuttil eṉakku vēṟṟumaiyillai. tāy moḻiyai ellāttuṟaiyilum mutaṉmaiyākak kaṟpatōṭu. cilavāṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ ayal moḻikaḷaiyum kaṟkum vāyppu irukka vēṇṭum. ayal moḻikaḷilum āṅkilam oṉṟukkē taṉi mutaṉmai koṭukkāmal uruciyam pireñcu, cerumaṉi, pāṉicu, cappāṉ mutalāṉa moḻikaḷaiyum intiyap piṟamoḻikaḷaiyum naṉku kaṟṟuk koḷḷum vāyppiṉai eṅkum vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum. eṉavē irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭil nam kalvik koḷkaikaḷ piṉvarumāṟu amaiyum eṉṟu etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ.
1. tamiḻakattil tamiḻ oṉṟē maḻalai mutal palkalai varai ettuṟaiyilum payiṟṟu moḻi.
2. tamiḻvaḻi māṇavarkkē ellā vēlai vāyppum uyar kalviyum.
3. intiyap piṟa moḻikaḷaiyum ayal moḻikaḷaiyum mēl vakuppukkaḷil naṉku kaṟṟu koḷvataṟku niṟainta vāyppum tiṭṭaṅkaḷum amaittal.
- mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkam


eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam eṅkē pōy muṭiyum?
tamiḻeḻuttuc cīrtiruttam paṟṟi arai nūṟṟāṇṭuk kālamāka eḻuntu varum karuttup pūcalkaḷ namakkut teriyum. tamiḻeḻuttu varivaṭivam kālantōṟum māṟi vantuḷḷa nīṇṭa varalāṟum namakkut teriyātataṉṟu. periyāriṉ `eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam’, tuṇaivēntar vāce. kuḻantaicāmiyiṉ `tamiḻ eḻuttuc cīramaippu’, koṭumuṭi caṇmukaṉāriṉ putiya tamiḻ vaṭivam, koṇṭal makātēvaṉāriṉ `eḻuttum karuttum’, ce.vai. caṇmukaṉāriṉ `eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam’, vīrā. irācakōpālaṉāriṉ tamiḻ eḻuttuc cīrtiruttap puraṭci’, tamiḻ moḻiyiyaṟ kaḻakattiṉ `eḻuttuc cīrtirutta malar’, veḷḷaiyāmpaṭṭucuntaraṉār tokutta `eḻuttuc cīrmai’ maṇavaiyāriṉ `kālam tēṭumtamiḻ’, cu. irattiṉacāmiyiṉ `tamiḻ eḻuttu varivaṭiva varalāṟu’ mutāṉa nūlkaḷum pala itaḻkaḷil eḻutiya aṟiñarkaḷiṉ kaṭṭuraikaḷuma nam urimaip pārvaikkum pakuttaṟivuc cintaṉaikkum uriyavai.
ikkāla vaḷarccikkeṉat tamiḻ eḻuttut tiruttam tēvaiyē illai eṉpavaraip paḻamaivātikaḷ eṉṟō, camutāya vaḷarcciyiṉ etirikaḷ eṉṟō, puriyāta piṟpōkkāḷarkaḷ eṉṟō, 21ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ vaḷarcci vēkattai aṟiyātavarkaḷ eṉṟō, tamiḻk kuḻantaikaḷiṉ eḻuttuc cumaiyaik kaṇṭum iraṅkātavarkaḷ eṉṟō, paḻippaṭṭam cūṭṭa vēṇṭiyatillai. atu pōla eḻuttut tiruttam virumpuvōrait tamiḻukku etirikaḷ eṉṟō, tamiḻp pakaivarkaḷiṉ kaiyāṭkaḷ eṉṟō, tamiḻ marapu aṟiyātavarkaḷ eṉṟō, aṟintum kaṟpaṉai vātikaḷ eṉṟō, tamiḻiṉ taṉittaṉmaiyaik keṭuppavarkaḷ eṉṟō iḷivaravu colla vēṇṭiyatumillai. ivaiyellām koṇṭa koḷkaiyai valiyuṟuttum aṟiñarkaḷ karuttu.
eḻuttuc cīrtiruttattukku yār yār icaintuḷḷaṉar, ātaravu nalkiṉar eṉṟa peyarivartam paṭṭiyalait tuṇaivēntar vā.ce.ku. avarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ittiruttattukku icaiyātōrum, vēṇṭām eṉpōrum palaruḷar. tamiḻāḻvār eṉap pōṟṟappeṟṟa moḻiyaṟiñar tēvanēyap pāvāṇar vaṭamoḻiyeḻuttuk kalappu tamiḻiṉ tūymaiyaik keṭukkum eṉavum tamiḻeḻuttu varivaṭiva māṟṟamum tamiḻukkut tēvaiyillai eṉavum aḻuttamākak kūṟiyavar. periyāriṉ vaḻittōṉṟalāṉa aṟiñar aṇṇā āṭcikku vanta piṉṉum periyāriṉ eḻuttu māṟṟattaip piṉpaṟṟavillai; kalaiñariṉ āṭcik kālattum itutāṉ nilai. muttamiḻk kāvalar ki.ā.pe. vicuvanātam, cilampuc celvar ma.po.ci., tīpam pārttacārati, peruñcittiraṉār, putuvait tuṇaivēntar vēṅkaṭacuppiramaṇiyaṉ eṉṟiṉṉa palar muṟaiyeḻuttu vaṭiviṉaiyē vēṇṭiṉar.
iṉṟum ilaṅkai malēciyā mutalāṉa veḷināṭṭut tamiḻiṉattār muṟaivaṭivaiyē kaṭaippiṭikkiṉṟaṉar. aracāṇaiyiṭṭa tirutta vaṭivaṅkaḷai ēṟṟuk koḷḷāta pala tamiḻitaḻkaḷum iṉṉum namatu nāṭṭil uḷa. kaṇicamāṉa potup perumakkaḷum itaṉaip piṉpaṟṟavillai.
āṇaiyeḻuttu
pāṭanūlkaḷē viṭāppiṭiyāka āṇaiyeḻuttaip payaṉ paṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. āṇaiyeḻuttil acciṭṭa nūlkaḷaittāṉ potu nūlakattil vāṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟa vitiyiṉāl, patippāḷikaḷ viṟpaṉai karuti ivveḻuttil acciṭukiṉṟaṉar. inta āṇaiyeḻuttāl pakkaṅkaḷ accuc celavukaḷ kūṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉpataṉai aṟintum niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ kaṭṭāyam piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu.
tantai periyār 1935il kāṭṭiya eḻuttut tiruttaṅkaḷai arai nūṟṟāṇṭuk kālam ēṟṟuk koḷḷavillai eṉṟāl, avarmēl aṉpillai eṉpatu poruḷaṉṟu. avartam camutāyap pōrāṭṭak koḷkaikaḷai ēṟṟu ottuḻaitta toṇṭarkaḷkūṭa moḻi paṟṟiya inta oṉṟiṉai mēṟkoḷḷa muyalavillai. ataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ: pala māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceyvataṟku inta eḻuttu māṟṟam tēvaiyillai eṉa oru cārār karutiṉar. oru taṭavai eḻuttu varivaṭivil kaivaikka iṭaṅkoṭuttu viṭṭāl, yār yārō avaravarm ātikkattiṟ kēṟpa, kaivaittuk koṇṭēyiruppar: toṭarcciyiṉṟi moḻiccitaivum, moḻikkulaivum, nūlaḻivūm ēṟpaṭum eṉa orucārār añciṉar. iḵtu iṉṟaikkum poruntum.
periyār nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāviṉ niṉaivāka 19.10.1978il 15 eḻuttukaṭku māṟṟāṉa cīrtirutta eḻuttukkaḷait kāṭṭi aṉṟirunta aracu aracāṇai piṟappittatu. āṇaiyiṭṭa cila tiṅkaṭkuḷḷēyē, aikāramum oḷakāramum māṟiṉṟip paḻaiya vaṭivaṅkaḷilēyē irukkum eṉa aracu veḷiyiṭṭatu. itaṉai ōr eccarikkai eṉṟu koḷḷavēṇṭum.
tiruttak kāraṇaṅkaḷ
iṉṉa eḻuttukku iṉṉa eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭu amaiya vēṇṭum eṉṟa muyaṟciyai mēṟkoḷvataṟku muṉ, tiruttam vēṇṭikaḷ ataṟkuc collivarum kāraṇappāṭukaḷai mutaṟkaṇ nām ottuka koḷkiṉṟōmā? ottukkoḷḷa muṭiyumā? ottuk koṇṭāl, ivai tamiḻ moḻiyai eṅkuppōy eppaṭic cāykkum eṉṟu uṉṉippākap pārkkavēṇṭum. ik kāraṇat taṉmaikaḷai nōkkum pōtutāṉ ulakamalaikaḷai oṉṟu cērttālaṉṉa malaippu nammaip piṇikkiṉṟatu.
muṭinta nōkkam vēṟu: tamiḻaṉṟu
`oru moḻikku olitāṉ aṭippaṭai: eḻuttukkaḷ olikaṭku nām koṭukkum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ; oliyaṉkaḷai nām kuṟaikkavillai; varivaṭivu moḻikku oru karuviyē. vaṭivattai māṟṟuvatālō kuṟaippatālō moḻi pātikkappaṭuva tillai’ eṉpatu eḻuttut tiruttikaḷiṉ oru vātam. itu añcattakka vātam ākum. ivvātam kaṟkālattaic cārntatu: pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka oru moḻikkum ataṉ eḻuttu vaṭivukkum uyirum uṭalum pōṉṟa piṇaippu marapāka, varalāṟāka vantirukkiṟatu. ōr eḻuttu vaṭivaip pārttavuṭaṉ itu iṉṉa moḻi eṉṟu nām aṟintukoḷvatillaiyā? oru moḻiyiṉ olikkum ataṉ eḻuttukkum maraput toṭar pillai eṉṟāl, tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ olikaḷai enta eḻuttilum eḻutalām eṉṟa accamāṉa ōr uṭkaruttu itaṉuḷ aṭaṅkik kiṭakkiṟatu eṉpataṉai uṇartta virumpukiṟēṉ. tamiḻicaik kiḷarcciyil icaikku moḻi tēvaiyillai; enta moḻiyilum pāṭalām eṉṟa vātattiṟkum itaṟkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭā? `uyirkaḷ kollap paṭuvatillai; uṭalkaḷai yātum ceyyalām’ eṉṟa kītaiyiṉ pōkkē iṅku veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
inta muṭivu nōkkattai mēlum nām purintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. tamiḻ eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam vēṇṭunariṉ karuttukkaḷaiyum viḷakkaṅkaḷaiyum tokuttuk kaṇittup pārttāl, intiya nāṭṭiṉ orumaippāṭu nōkki, tēvanākari eḻuttukkaḷait tamiḻum mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟaivārum uḷar. ulakavorumaippāṭu karuti urōmaṉ eḻuttukkaḷaiyē tamiḻ ēṟṟukkoḷḷalām eṉavum itaṉāl āṅkilam kaṟkum ellāt tamiḻk kuḻantaikaṭkum oru moḻic cumai kuṟaiyum eṉavum valiyuṟuttum aṟiñarkaḷum uḷar. mēṟkuṟitta iruvakaiyiṉaruḷ urōmaṉ eḻuttuvātikaḷē kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ. `mummoḻik koḷkai ēṟkappaṭṭu atu vaṭa mānilaṅkaḷil ceyalpaṭuttappaṭumāyiṉ varivaṭiva eḷimai kāraṇamāka maṟṟa moḻikaḷai viṭat tamiḻ atika māṇavarkaḷāl kaṟkappaṭuvatu cāttiyamākum’ eṉpatu ōr aṟiñar karuttu. eṉavē ivar kaṭkellām tamiḻ eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam ōr iṭaikaḻi nōkkē eṉpatu maṟukkavō maṟaikkavō iyalāta uṇmai. iṉṟaiya aṟiviyal yukattil evvakaiyālum tamiḻiṉ tūymaiyum, taṉittaṉmaiyum iṉṉōrkkuk kacappākum. mutalvar kalaiñar karuṇānitiyār piṉvarumāṟu eḻutukiṉṟār. `tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷaic cīrtiruttam ceyyum pērāl tamiḻaiyē cīrtiruttam ceyyappōvatākap paṟaicāṟṟit tiripavarkaḷuma, intap pōrvaiyil tamiḻiṉ oli-ōcaiyaikkūṭa māṟṟiyamaikkum ceyalkaḷil tuṇintiṟaṅkum tōḻarkaḷum keṭuttu viṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. oliyai maṭṭum aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, tamiḻai rōmaṉ lipiyil māṟṟiṉālkūṭap paravāyillai eṉṟu collum āpattāṉa vātamum cilarāl tuṇintu eḻutappaṭukiṉṟatu. eṉavē kalaiñariṉ karuttu  eḻuttut tiruttikaḷiṉ uḷnōkkattai veḷippaṭuttak kāṇpīrkaḷ.
tuṇai vēntar vā.ce. kuḻantaicāmi tamiḻ eḻuttuca cīramaippukkuk kūṟum attaṉai kāraṇakkūṟukaḷum urōmaṉ eḻuttiṉ aṭittaḷattaik koṇṭavai. cerumāṉiya, intōnēciya, malēciya moḻikaḷ ellām urōmaṉ vaṭivattaip peṟṟu vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa eṉa avar eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvar. `eppaṭiyō uyirmey vaṭivaṅkaḷai uruvākkuvatil eṇṇaṟṟa cikkalkaḷai nām uruvākkik koṇṭōm eṉavum, `urōmaṉ varivaṭivattōṭu oppiṭumpoḻutu namatu varivaṭivattiṉ cikkalkaḷ puriyum’ eṉavum avar colvati liruntē tamiḻukku avar koṇṭa aḷavukōl urōmaṉ eṉpatu teḷivākum. uyirum meyyum eṉṟa iraṇṭōṭu uyirmey eṉṟa mūṉṟāvatu kūṭṭuṟavaik kaṇṭatum, ataṟkeṉat taṉikkuṟiyīṭukaḷ koṇṭatum tamiḻ moḻikkē yuriya tirāviṭaviyalākum. it taṉmaiyai vaṭamoḻiyum vaṭa vintiya moḻikaḷum ēṟṟukkoṇṭapiṉ, atu intiya moḻikaḷiṉ taṉmaiyāka virintatu. `ittirāviṭaviyal cikkalāṉatu. aṟiviyal vaḷarccikku utavātatu’ eṉṟu vā.ce. kuvum avar pōṉṟa eḻuttu māṟṟam vēṇṭunarum colvataṟku aṭittaḷam, urōmaṉai aḷavukōlākak koṇṭatākum. uyirmey eṉṟa kūṭṭunilaiyai nām uṭaṉpaṭāviṭṭāl, tamiḻ moḻikkēyaṉṟi intiya moḻikaṭkum uṭaṉpaṭā viṭṭāl ilakkaṇattil eṉṉa citaivukaḷ ēṟpaṭum eṉak kaṟpaṉai ceyyavum iyalātu. eḻuttut tiruttikaṭku itu paṟṟiya kavalai ili.
urōmaṉ aḷavai
karuvikaṭkēṟpat tamiḻeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷaic cīramaippuc ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu collum aṟiñar vā.ce. kuḻantaicāmi kuṟiyīṭu eṉpatu uyir, meyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ pātiyaḷavu akalamuḷḷatākavum iru iṇaikōṭukaṭkuḷ aṭaṅkuvatākavum irukkavēṇṭum eṉavum neṟi kūṟuvar. inta aḷaviyal ulaka moḻikaḷiṉ potumaiyā? enta moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil inta aḷavu muṟai tamiḻukku ilakkaṇamākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu? mēlum iruiṇaik kōṭṭiṟkuḷ tamiḻ varivaṭivukaḷai aṭakkiviṭṭāl poṟivaḻi acciṟkum taṭṭaccu, tolaipēci, miṉṉiyal tolaipēci ākiya vaṟṟiṟkum peritum tuṇai ceyyum eṉpatu iṉṉōrtam karuttu.
- mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkam
akattiṇai pākupāṭu
akapporuḷ puṟapporuḷ eṉṉum tamiḻ nūlvakai iraṇṭaṉuḷ ciṟanta akattiṇaiyāyvu iṉip paṉmuṟaiyāṉ mēṟ koḷḷappaṭum. āyvukkiṭai ettaṉaiyō karuttu māṟāṭṭaṅkaḷ vallavaṉ iṭṭa muṭiccuppōlat tōṉṟi nammai malaivikkiṉṟaṉa; kuḻantaikkup pōṭum putirkḷ pōla nammaṟivaic cōtikkiṉṟaṉa. kayiṟu ceyvōṉ muṭiccōṭu ceyvatillai. ataṉaip payaṉ koḷvār tam aṟiyāmai yālum tuṇiccalālum muṭiccukkaḷ paṭṭuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. “putirkaḷ pōṭuvārkkēṉum viḷaṅkavēṇṭumallavā?” cilarkkup putirkaḷ iṭat teriyumēyaṉṟi avaṟṟai viṭatteriyā. iṅṅaṉamāka akattiṇaikkuk kālantōṟum ēṟiya muṭiccukkaḷum palavāyiṉa. ippaṭi muṭiccukkaḷai - kaṭum putirkaḷaik kaṇṭu malaiyātum, iṭaiyil tōṉṟiya muṭiccu eṭuttup putirviṭuttup pōvatutāṉ āymuṟai eṉṟu mayaṅkātum, āyvāḷaṉ akattiṇai mūla ilakkiyattōṭu nēraṭi aṟivut toṭarpukoḷḷum aṭiyavaṉ pōlac cemporuḷ kāṇpāṉ. marapiyal, nūliyal, aṟiviyal, uḷaviyal paṟṟic colluṅkālait tam muyaṟcikku ēṟpa akattiṇai aiyaṅkaḷ akaṉṟoḻiyak kāṇpāṉ. tolpaḻam paṭaippāṉa akavilakkiyattiṉ uḷḷaṅkāṇa muyalum namakku, iṟṟait tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ, nākarikattiṉ vēṟupāṭṭāl aiyappāṭu pala tōṉṟutal iyalpiṉum iyalpē. aiyattōṟṟam uṇmait tōṟṟattukku vaḻi kāṭṭum ātaliṉ, akattiṇaik kalviyāḷarkku uṇṭām aiyakkūṟukaḷai mutaṟkaṇ moḻikuval.
(a) akattiṉ ilakkaṇam yātu? akappāṭṭu eṉpatu kātaṟ pāṭṭāyiṉ, kātaṟṟiṇai eṉṟu peyar vaittirukkalāmē? akattiṇai eṉap peyariyatēṉ? akam kātal eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ paṉmoḻiyā? iṭaikkāla uraiyāciriyarkaḷum ikkālap pulavar perumakkaḷum akattiṟkuk kūṟivarum ilakkaṇam takavuṭaiyatā?
(ā) akattiṇai eḻuvakaippaṭum eṉṟa pākupāṭu icaiyumā? ivveṇṇikkaitāṉum piḻaiyeṉat tōṟṟavillaiyā? caṅkavilakkiyattu 1862 akappākkaḷuḷ kaikkiḷaikku nāṉkē, peruntiṇaikkup pattē uriyavākak kaṟkiṉṟōmē! kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikaḷ akamāyiruppavum, caṅkac cāṉṟōr ivaṟṟaip pōṟṟip pāṭal pala ceyyāmaikku ētuveṉṉaiyō? tamiḻ ilakkiyamellām akam puṟam eṉṉum vakaiyiraṇṭāy aṭaṅkum eṉṟa karutturai ēṟpuṭaittā?
(i) tamiḻ moḻikkaṇ akattiṇai nūl piṟattaṟku utaviya cūḻnilaikaḷ yāvai? akattiṇaikku vitikaḷ vakuttār yāvar? oppaṟṟa aṟivu muṉaivaṉ oruvaṉ itu viti, itu marapu eṉṟu kaṟpittaruḷiṉaṉō? aṉṟip palar kūṭi āynta aṟivuk kāṭciyiṉ viḷaivō? akavilakkiyam ilakkiya vulakiṟku oru taṉittamiḻ ñāyiṟu eṉavum, eṇṇam nuṇṇiya tamiḻaṟi viṉōr akalakkaruti āḻaniṉaintu paṭaittaḷitta ilakkiyap putukkōḷ eṉavum colli makiḻvōmēl, aṉṉa putupapaṭaippiṉaic ceytaṟku uriya nōkkam eṉṉa? annōkkam aṟavaḻippaṭṭatā? talaivaṉ taṉ kaṟpuṭai nallāḷai aḻavaittup parattai vēṭṭam nāṭukiṉṟāṉ; inta illaṟa vaḻukkalai oru tiṇaipporuḷākak koṇṭu akattiṇai pāṭukiṉṟatē! ituvā nōkkam? ituvā aṟam?
(ī) pāṭuvārkku ērāḷamāṉa vitikaḷai immiyum vaḻuvalākā marapeṉa vitikkum akavilakkaṇam kaṟpaṉai yūṟṟukkuk kallākātō? aṟivuk kūrmaiyai maḻukkum aram ākātō? avaiyakkāla akappulavarkaḷ tolkāppiyam kūṟum akavitikaḷai muḻutum piṉpaṟṟiṉarkol? muraṇiyaṉavum perukkiyaṉavum uḷavō?
ivvāṟu akattiṇai kuṟittu aiyaviṉākkaḷai aṭukkic collutalō eḷitu; aṟivokkum viṭait teḷivu kāṇpatu attuṇai eḷitaṉṟēṉum, kāṇa muyalvatum muyalvippatum ivvārāycciyiṉ pāṭākum. caṅka vilakkiyamē nām peṟṟirukkum. tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷa toṉmaiyāṉa akavilakkiyam, ilakkiya ilakkaṇa ārāycci, kātalarkaḷ pōla tammuḷ pirintu nillātavai; pirikka muṭiyātavai; valintu pirittāl vaḷappaṭātavai. ātaliṉ, akavilakkiyamum akavilakkaṇamum caṅkanūṟ celvaṅkaḷiṉ peruntuṇai koṇṭu oruṅku ārāytaṟkuriyaṉa. āyuntōṟum mēleṇṇiya aiyac cikkalkaḷ oruvāṟu aṟupaṭak kāṇpīr.
akattiṇaip pirivukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭai
1. kaikkiḷai 2. kuṟiñci 3. mullai 4. marutam 5. neytal 6. pālai, 7. peruntiṇai eṉappaṭṭa ēḻum akattiṇaip pākupāṭām. ivaṟṟuḷ kuṟiñci mutal pālai iṟutiyākiya iṭainiṉṟa aintum aintiṇaiyeṉa oru kūṭṭāka vaḻaṅkappaṭum. ippirivukaḷukku aṭinilaik kāraṇam yātu? kaikkiḷaiyāvatu orutalaikkāmam eṉṟum, aintiṇaiyāvatu otta kāmam eṉṟum, peruntiṇaiyāvatu ovvākkāmam eṉṟum aṟintōr colluvar. iṅṅaṉam kāmat taṉmaikaḷ aṭippaṭai ākumāyiṉ, aintiṇaiyai orē pirivākak koṇṭu, akattiṇaip pirivukaḷ mūṉṟeṉal poruntu mēyaṉṟi  ēḻeṉal poruntātukāṇ. mēlum, kaikkiḷai peruntiṇai eṉṉum peyarkkuṟikaḷ tam kāmattaṉmai cuṭṭum kai, peru, aṭaikaḷ peṟṟuḷa. atu voppa aṉputtiṇai eṉṟa peyaraṉṟō aintiṇaikku varutal vēṇṭum? aintiṇai eṉa eṇṇaṭaiyiruttal okkumā? ātaliṉ akappirivukaṭkuk kāmattaṉmai aṭippaṭaiyākātu.
tamiḻ akattiṇai kūṟum palvēṟu kātaṟ kūṟukaḷ tamiḻakattiṉ palvēṟu nilakkūṟukaḷaic cārntu eḻuntavai eṉa oru kōṭpāṭu uḷatu.1 nam tāyakattup pālai eṉṟu cuṭṭik kāṭṭattakka nilappāl illai (tāl, 947) kuṟiñci, mullai, marutam, neytal eṉṟa nāṟṟiṇaikaḷukkē iyalpāṉa nilappāṅkukaḷ uḷa. itaṉāl nāṉilam eṉpatu ulakiṟku oru peyarāyiṟṟu. pālaikku iyalnilam iṉṟēṉum, tirinilam uṇṭu eṉṟum, kāṭum malaiyum kōṭait tī veppattāl tattam pacumai iḻanta tirinilaiyē pālai eṉṟum cilappatikāram naṉku teḷivupaṭuttum.
vēṉalaṅ kiḻavaṉoṭu veṅkatir vēntaṉ
tāṉalan tirukat taṉmaiyiṟ kuṉṟi
mullaiyum kuṟiñciyum muṟaimaiyiṟ ṟirintu
nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
pālai yeṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum (cilap. 15, 95-9)
oru kālavēṟṟumaiyāl iraṇṭu tiṅkaḷ iyalpumāṟit tōṉṟum nilacceyaṟkaiyē pālaittiṇaikku iṭamām. eṉiṉum, nilaiyā nīrillā nilattiliruntu nīṭittu nilaikkum paṉṉūṟu pālaip pāṭalkaḷai viḷaivittukakoṇṭa caṅka kālac collēruḻavarkaḷiṉ ilakkiya uḻavu aṟiviṉ koḻumuṉaikku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu. iṅṅaṉ pālait tiṇaikku oruvakaiyāṉa nilam uṇṭeṉṟu koḷḷiṉum kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikaṭku evvāṟṟāṉum nilam illai eṉpatu nūṉmuṭivu. ātaliṉ eḻutiṇai akappirivu nilavaṭip paṭai koṇṭatu eṉṟālum poruntāmai kāṇka.
tiṇai ēḻāyiṉamaikkuk kāraṇam mukkāmattaṉmaiyum aṉṟu; nāṉilap pāṅkum aṉṟu eḻuvakaik kātaloḻukkaṅ kaḷē uripporuḷkaḷē - ām, eṉṟu aṟivōmāka. ivvoḻukkaṅkaḷ taṉi nilaikoṇṭavai; tammuḷ toṭarpu vēṇṭi nillātavai eṉpatu nīḷa niṉaiyattakum oru ciṟappiyalpu. ātalālaṉṟō kuṟiñci mullai, marutam, neytal, pālai eṉpavai otta kāmattaṉmaiyāl oṉṟāyiṉum, aintiṇai eṉat taṉinilai kuṟikkum eṇṇup peyar peṟṟaṉa. kuṟiñci mutalāya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ avvannilattukku uriya malarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷē eṉṟum, ippeyarkaḷ piṉṉar uripporuḷkaḷaip pulappaṭuttum kuṟiyīṭukaḷāyiṉa eṉṟum karutuvar oru cārār2. aṟṟaṉṟu eṉa maṟuttuk kuṟiñci mutalāyavai puṇartal mutalāya uripporuḷkaḷai mutaṟkaṇ taruvaṉa eṉavum piṉṉarē malarkaḷaiyum nilaṅkaḷaiyum kuṟikkappōntaṉa eṉavum karutuvar maṟṟoru cārār. mutaṟporuḷ etu? vaḻipporuḷ etu? eṉat tuṇivatil iṉṉōr māṟupaṭuvar. iruvakaip poruḷum kuṟiñci mutalām coṟkaḷukku muṉṉō piṉṉō uṇṭu eṉpatilum, akattiṇaiyilakkaṇam vakutta nāḷil iruporuḷum vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpatilum iṉṉōr vēṟupaṭṭilar. eṉiṉum kaikkiḷai mutalāp peruntiṇai iṟuvāy eḻutiṇai eṉa eṇṇuṅkāl, kuṟiñci mutalām coṟkaḷ tarum, taravēṇṭum poruḷkaḷ eṉṉa? nilamillāk kaikkiḷai peruntiṇai kaḷōṭu eṇṇappaṭutaliṉ, ivaṟṟukku nilapporuḷ ceytal ākātu; uripporuḷ kōṭalē muṟai eṉpatu teḷivu. aintiṇaiyuḷ aṭaṅkiya ovvoṉṟum taṉit tiṇaiyākak karutappaṭṭamaiyiṉ, avaiyap pulavarkaḷ aintiṇai mēlum pāṭātu āḷukku oru tiṇaiyāka ilakkiyam kaṇṭaṉar.
marutamō rampōki neytalam mūvaṉ
karutuṅ kuṟiñci kapilaṉ - karutiya 
pālaiyōta lāntai paṉimullai pēyaṉē
nūlaiyō taiṅkuṟu nūṟu.
eṭṭuttokaiyuḷ aiṅkuṟunūṟum kalittokaiyum pāṭiṉōr eṇṇikkai kuṟainta nūlkaḷ. āṇṭum tiṇaikkoru āciriyarāṉa ceytiyai - aintiṇaiyaiyum ōrāciriyaṉ pāṭā muṟaiyai mēlai veṇpākkaḷ aṟivikkuma.
peruṅkaṭuṅkōṉ pālai kapilaṉ kuṟiñci
marutaṉiḷa nākaṉ marutam aruñcōḻaṉ
nallut tiraṉmullai nallan tuvaṉeytal
kalvivalārta kaṇṭa kali.
peruṅkaṭukkō pālaittiṇai (pirivu) oṉṟaṉaiyē pāṭip `pālai pāṭiya’ eṉṟa ciṟappup peṟṟavar. oru tiṇaikkuriya uripporuḷaip pāṭuṅkālum, ellāt tuṟai mēlum pāṭātu oru tuṟaiyaḷavil pāṭiniṉṟa akap pulavar palar. veṟi pāṭiya kāmakkaṇṇiyār oru tuṟai pāṭiya ciṟappup pulavar. mēlum mutal karupporuḷkaḷaic ciṟitum kūṟātē uripporuḷ maṭṭum kūṟum akappāṭalkaḷum palavuḷa.
nōmeṉ ṉeñcē nōmeṉ ṉeñcē
imaitīyp paṉṉa kaṇṇir tāṅki
amaitaṟ kamaintanaṅ kātalar
amaivila rākutal nōmeṉ ṉeñcē		(kuṟun 4)
uripporuḷ oṉṟē nuvalum ivvaṉaiya pākkaḷ pālaittiṇai, kuṟiñcittiṇaiyeṉac cevvitiṉ peyar peṟukiṉṟaṉa. kāraṇam tiṇaimaiyāvatu uripporuḷē paṟṟiyatu. mutalum karuvumō apporuṭkut tuṇaimaiyām attuṇaiyē. ātaliṉ akattiṇai eḻumaip pakuppu kātalarkaḷiṉ kaikkiḷai puṇartal pirital iruttal iraṅkal ūṭalperuntiṇai eṉṉum kāmavoḻukkaṅkaḷai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭatu eṉṟu teḷiyattakum akattiṉ ilakkaṇam eṉṉai? akam eṉṟa kiḷavikkup poruḷ eṉṉai? ivai iṉit toṭarntu āyavēṇṭupavai eṉiṉum, ippōtu āyvu peṟumāṟillai. iṭaiyē cilapala akacceytikaḷ viḷakkam peṟṟa piṉpu. ivvāyvait toṭaṅkutu teḷivukkum muṭipukkum eḷimai ceyyum.
akattiṇaiyum aintiṇaiyum
akattiṇaip paṭaippil kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikaḷai viṭa aintiṇai pallāṟṟāṉum mēlāyatu eṉpataṟku uṟaḻkūṟṟu vēṇṭā. akattiṇait tollāciriyar tolkāppiyar kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷai iruvēṟu taṉicciṟu cūttiraṅkaḷ aḷavil (995-996) akattiṇaiyiyal iṟutik kaṇṇē colli amaikuvar. `kaikkiḷai mutalāp peruntiṇai iṟuvāy’ (949) eṉa eṇṇuṅkāl mutaliṭam peṟṟa kaikkiḷaikūṭap poruḷ kūṟuṅkāl avviṭam peṟāmai karutattakum. `kaikkiḷaik kuṟippē’, `peruntiṇaik kuṟippē eṉṟu ivviru tiṇaikaḷiṉ ciṟunilai tōṉṟa avaṟṟiṉ poruṭ cūttiraṅkaḷai muṭittuk kāṭṭuvar. `kuṟippu’ eṉṟa coṟpeyvāl ivviru tiṇaikaḷ virittuppāṭum peṟṟiyavalla eṉṟu cuṭṭuvar. itaṉāl tolkāppiyam aintiṇaiyē akattiṇai eṉpatupōla, aintiṇaiyiṉ ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷaip palapaṭa viritturaippak kāṇalām. akattiṇaiyiyal 55 nūṟpākkaḷaik koṇṭatu. akattiṇai eṉap potup peyar pūṇṭiruntum, itaṉ 50 nūṟpākkaḷ aintiṇaiyiṉ neṟikaḷaiyē virittu moḻital kāṇka. kaḷavil kaṟpiyal poruḷiyal meyppāṭṭiyal eṉappaṭum piṟa nāṉku iyalkaḷuṅkūṭa aintiṇai nuvalum amaippiṉavākavē uḷa. aintiṇaik kātal aṟanalattatu. ulakam oppuvatu, makkaṭku iyalvatu, ilakkiyattukku icaintatu. iṉṉa naṉṉayaṅkaḷai nōkkic caṅkap pulamaiyiṉōr aintiṇait tuṟaikaḷaiyē peritum pāṭiṉar. tolkāppiyar ceytatu pōlak kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikaṭku uriya otukkiṭam nalkiṉar. ippāṅkiṉpaṭi, aintiṇai peritum, ēṉai irutiṇai ciṟitumāka innūlilum ārāyccippaṭum.
aintiṇai ivvaḷavu mēlān tarattateṉiṉum, akattiṇai aintiṇai eṉṟa coṟkaḷ oruporuṭ paṉmoḻiyākā eṉpatu aṟika. ivaṟṟai oṉṟeṉak karutiyum, kaikkiḷai perun tiṇaikaḷai akattiṉ vēṟeṉak karutiyum vaḷarnta eḻuttellām perum piḻai. “akattiṇaiyiṉkaṇ kaikkiḷai varutal tiṇai mayakkām piṟaveṉiṉ, kaikkiḷai mutaṟ peruntiṇai yiṟuvāya eḻutiṇaiyiṉuḷḷum kaikkiḷaiyum peruntiṇaiyum akattaic cārnta puṟamāyiṉum” (pā. 4) eṉṉum tirukkōvai yuraiyāciriyar karutturai ippiḻaiyiṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. orukuṭip piṟanta pallōruḷ cilar ciṟappuṟṟum maṟṟum cilar ciṟappiṉṟiyum iruntataṟkāka, aṉṉōr oru kuṭippiṟappukku iḻukkuṇṭō? aintiṇai nōkkak kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikaḷ ciṟappilavākalām; eṉiṉum, avviraṇṭum eḻutiṇai eṉappaṭṭa akattiṇai yātaṟku iḻukkillai.
kaikkiḷai mutalāp peruntiṇai yiṟuvāy
muṟpaṭak kiḷanta eḻutiṇai yeṉpa	 (tol, 946)
eṉṟa toṉṉeṟippaṭi, akattiṉ mutalākavum iṟutiyākavum eṇṇuppeṟṟa kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikaḷai `akattaic cārnta puṟam’ eṉṟu kōvaiyuraiyāciriyar kuṟippatu aṭi muraṇillaiyā? ivviraṇṭaiyum puṟamākkiya avar `akattai” eṉṟa collāl muḻutum aintiṇaiyaittāṉē karutiviṭṭār eṉpatu pulappaṭavillaiyā? kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikaḷiṉ perumaippāṭu evvāṟāyiṉum, avaṟṟai akattiṇaip pakuppiṉiṉṟu taḷḷa muṭiyāmaikkum, aintiṇaiyōṭu uṭaṉeṇṇu taṟkum kuruti yoppaṉṉa paṇpoppiṉai ivaṟṟiṭait tamiḻ mūtāḷar kaṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. avvoppiṉai piṉṉiyalkaḷil virippōm. ivviraṇṭaiyum cērttu koḷḷāviṭiṉ, akattiṇai nakamaṟṟa viralpōlavum paṭiyaṟṟa maṉaipōlavum muḻuvaṉappil oru kuṟaivupaṭaiyatākat tōṉṟum eṉa īṇṭuk kuṟippiṭal pōtum, ēḻu tiṇaikaḷaiyum oruṅku cuṭṭa vēṇṭuṅkāl, akattiṇai eṉṉum potuk kūṟiyīṭṭai āḷutal tolkāppiya vaḻakku.
puṟattiṇai maruṅkiṟ porunti ṉallatu
akattiṇai maruṅkiṉ aḷavuta vilavē.			(tol. 1000)

akattiṇai maruṅkiṉ ariltapa vuṇarntōr.
puṟattiṇai yilakkaṇam tiṟappaṭak kiḷappiṉ		(tol. 1001)
akattiṇaip potuppeyarai aintiṉaip piriviṉ maṟu peyarākat tolkāppiyam āṇṭatillai. “makkaḷ nutaliya ataṉ aintiṇai” (999) “aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇai (1037) eṉṟāṅku uriya pirivup peyarāl vitantu collum naṭainiṉaiyattakum. `akaṉ aintiṇai’ eṉṟa potuvaṭaiyāl, aintiṇai akat tiṇaiyuḷ oruvakai eṉpatum, akakkaikkiḷai akapperun tiṇai eṉap piṟavakaikaḷum uḷa eṉpatum koḷḷakkiṭattal kāṇka. “aṉpiṉ akattiṇai kaḷaveṇappaṭuvatu eṉaṉātu” eṉṉātu, “aṉpiṉ aintiṇai kaḷaveṉappaṭuvatu” eṉṟa iṟaiyaṉārum “akat tuṟaikaḷ tāṅki aintiṇai neṟiyaḷāvi” eṉṟu viḻipapōṭu coṟ peytalai oppu nōkkavēṇṭum. ātaliṉ kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikaḷ kuṟainta pāṭalkaḷ uṭaiyaṉavē yeṉiṉum akat tiṇai ārāycciyil iṭampeṟa vēṇṭiyavai eṉpatu teḷivu.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	The Ancient Tamils, Part 1: p 46. S.K. Pilllai.
2.	tol-poruḷ. ru. iḷampūraṇam; tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāra ārāycci pa. 24; paḻantamiḻar nākarikam; pa. 18. ḷuvaraṉanaiḷa in kūyaaṭai ṭuvainacayavaracana yaṉē ḻaḷaivaṭicala ña. 273.
3.	iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷ. pavāṉantar kaḻakap patippu, pa. 25 tol. poruḷ. 5. nacciṉārkkiṉiyam; ṭākṭar pāratiyār. tol, akam, pāyiram iḷavaḻakaṉār akattiṇaiyiyal viḷakkam, pa. 87-89. aṭikkuṟippu.
- mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkam 
putiya pārvai juṉ 2016


akattiṇai pākupāṭu
1. aintiṇai - kaḷaviyal- iyaṟkaip puṇarcci
akattiṇaiyiṉ peruṅkūṟāṉa aintiṇaik kātalkaḷai iṉi ārāyvōm. caṅka akappāṭalkaḷaic cāṉṟākak koṇṭu aintiṇait tuṟaikaḷiṉ tiṟaṉ kāṇpōm. kaḷavoḻukkam kaṟpoḻukkam eṉa aintiṇai irupāṟpaṭum eṉṟu potuvākak kūṟiṉālum, kuṟiñcikkaḷavu kuṟiñcik kaṟpu, pālaikkaḷavu pālaikkaṟpu, mullaikkaḷavu mullaikkaṟpu, marutakkaḷavu marutakkaṟpu, neytaṟkaḷavu neytaṟkaṟpu eṉat tiṇaitōṟum iraṇṭum koḷḷattakum. iṅṅaṉam koḷvatāl kaḷavukkātaluma kaṟpukkātalum ellā nilattilum nikaḻvaṉavē eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkam peṟappaṭum.
kātalukkuk kāraṇam
kāmappuruvam eytiya naṅkai, tāy ēvaṟpaṭi tiṉaippuṉam celkiṉṟāḷ. tōḻiyarum uṭaṉcelkiṉṟaṉar. muṟṟiya katirkaḷaik kavarum kiḷikkūṭṭattait taṭṭaik karuvikoṇṭu veruṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. aruviyāṭiyum taḻai koytum malar toṭuttum vantu acōkantaṇṇiḻalil amarkiṉṟaṉar (kuṟiñcip, 39, 102) kumarapparuvam eytiya nampi taṉ vēṭṭaināyoṭu malaiyelām cuṟṟit tirikiṟāṉ. pal maṇam kamaḻum talaimālai aṇintu ivvēṭṭuvak kumaraṉ naṅkai irukkumiṭattukkut tāṉāka vantu cērukiṟāṉ (akam. 28). taṉ ampāl puṇpaṭṭa matayāṉai ippakkam pērntatuṇṭō? pōka nīvir kaṇṭatuṇṭō? eṉṟu malarūtum vaṇṭiṉ icaikkuc cevi koṭuttirunta iḷam peṇkaḷai ucāvukiṟāṉ (akam. 388) appōtu avaṉukkuk kātaṟ kuṟippu illai. mārpil ceṅkaḻunīrum talaiyil veṭcippūvum akattuc cantaṉap pūccum kaiyil villampum koṇṭu muṉṉiṟkum iḷaiñaṉukku muṉṉē iḷamātarkaḷ niṟkak kūciṉar. viṉāvukku maṟumoḻi aṉṉōr nāveḻavillai. itaṟkūṭē nampiyiṉ kaṇṇum naṅkaiyiṉ kaṇṇum naṭpukkoṇṭaṉa. kātalāṭiṉa; kāmattīppaṟṟiṉa. (akam. 48).
kaṇṭatum kātal eṉpatu ituvē. “kaṇ taravanta kāma oḷḷeri” (kuṟuta 305) eṉṟu kuppaik kōḻiyār kāmattiṉ piṟappiṭamākak kaṇṇai kūṟuvar. kalloṭu kal cērntu tī uṇṭāvatupōlat kaṇṇoṭu kaṇṇum cērntu tī uṇṭām; atu kāmattīyām eṉṟu ippulavar kuṟippippar. kal piṟappitta tī pañcu mutalāṉa paṟṟu poruḷ illaiyāyiṉ vaḷarātu. kaṇ piṟappitta kāmattī tāṉēyum vaḷaravallatu eṉpatai `oḷḷeri’ eṉṉum aṭaiyāl aṟiyalām. kaṇkaḷ kalantapiṉ kātalartam uḷḷaṅkaḷ kalakkiṉṟaṉavā? muṉṉarē uḷḷaṅ kalantamaiyaik kāṭṭum aṟikuṟiyā kaṇkalappu?
nāṭṭam iraṇṭum aṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk
kūṭṭi yuraikkum kuṟippurai yākum 	(tol. 1041)
oṉṟupaṭṭa uḷḷakkuṟippait talaivaṉ pārvai talaivikkum, avaḷ pārvai avaṉukkum uraikkiṉṟaṉa. oru karuttai vāyāṟ coṉṉāl veḷippaṭaiyāka naṉku viḷaṅkum. vāyāṟ collik koḷḷutal kātalulakiṟkup poruntumā? kātal nōyai vaḷarkkumā? ātaliṉ kātalarkaḷ uḷḷakkaruttaik kaṇṇāṟ collikkoḷpa aḵtu avarkaṭku vāyāṟ collik koḷvatupōlap puriyum. vāyiṉ ceyalaik kaṇ ceytalāl, “nāṭṭam iraṇṭum uraiyākum” eṉpar tolkāppiyar. kātaṟ kaḷattil kaṇṇukkuriya matippu ciṟitum vāykkillai. “vāyccoṟkaḷ eṉṉa payaṉum ila” eṉpar tiruvaḷḷuvar. kaṇ tanta kāmam eṉpataṟkākak kāmattukkuka kaṇ kāraṇamāyviṭumā? vīraṉaippeṟṟa vayiṟu (“īṉṟa vayiṟō ituvē” puṟam 89) eṉṟāl, vīraṉ piṟappukku vayiṟu kāraṇam eṉṟu colla māṭṭōm. ātaliṉ uḷḷak kāmam kaṇṇil veḷippaṭṭatu eṉpatallatu kāmattukkum kaṇṇukkum piṟapput toṭarpiṉṟu. pāl vēṟupaṭṭa āṇmaiyum peṇmaiyum pārttatum uḷḷam paṟikoṭuttuk kāmap pittēṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉkol? ivviṉā iṉṉum tīrā viṉāvō. kātal kāraṇam eṉṟu eḷitākac colli viṭukiṉṟōm. kātal eṉpatutāṉ yātu? itaṟku aṟiviyalum viṭai kūṟa oṇṇāmaiyaik keṉṉattu vākkar `pāluṭaliyal’ nūlil oppukiṟār: “pāluṇarvupaṟṟi oru poruttamāṉa koḷkaiyai nām kūṟuvataṟkiyalavillai eṉpatai ottukkoḷḷavēṇṭum. peṇṇoruttiyaik kāṇkiṉṟāṉ; avaḷ moḻiyaik kēṭkiṉṟāṉ; avaḷ maṇattai mōkkiṉṟāṉ. avaḷ meyyait tīṇṭukiṉṟāṉ. kaṇṭu kēṭṭu uṇṭu uyirttu uṟṟu aṟiyum pōtu uḷḷam kāmattiṉ koḷkalam ākiṉṟatu. uṭampil cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. ivaiyellām puṇarcciyiṟpōy muṭīvaṭaiyum kātal puṟanilaikaḷaik kaṇṭu kūṟumaḷavē aṟiviyal ceyyakkūṭiyatu. ataṉ tōṟṟattai itu teḷivākkumā? illavē illai.” ērāḷamāṉa iḷaiya āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum nāḷtōṟum cālaiyilum cōlaiyilum oruvaraiyoruvar kāṇkiṉṟaṉarē, kaṇṭum, oruvaṉ yārō oruttimēl kātal koḷkiṉṟāṉ; orutti evaṉō oruttaṉpāl kaṇcāykkiṉṟāḷ. palarai vilakki oruvarait tērntu virumpum icciṟappup pārvaikkuk kāraṇam ituveṉa uyiriyal nūlum colla muṭivatillai. iḵtu.
āyvukku uriya ariya cikkal eṉṟu nam muṉṉōrum karutiṉar. vāḷāvirātu kāraṇaṅkāṇa muyaṉṟaṉar. āṇ peṇ eṉpaṉa paṭaipput toṭakkattu orē oru poruḷām eṉṟum, teyva cāpattāl piḷavupaṭṭu iraṇṭāyiṉa eṉṟum, aṉṟu mutal oṉṟāy iṇaiyap pāṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kātalukku viḷakkam kūṟuvar piḷēṭṭō. itu umaiyoru pākam aruttanārīcuvaraṉ. eṉac civaṉukku āṇpāti peṇpāti uruvam kaṟpikkum intup purāṇattaip pōṉṟatu.
oṉṟē vēṟē eṉṟiru pālvayiṉa
oṉṟi uyarnta pāla tāṇaiyiṉ
otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum kāṇpa.	(tol. 1038)
uyarnta pāliṉ āṇaiyāl. nallūḻiṉ ēvalāl kātaṟ kāṭci nikaḻum eṉpar tolkāppiyar. kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyaik kāṇpāṉ eṉṟō, kiḻatti kiḻavaṉaik kāṇpāḷ eṉṟō
eḻuvāyum ceyappaṭu poruḷumākat tolkāppiyam naṭai toṭukkavillai. muntik kātalittavar yār? eṉṟa kātaliyalpu pulappaṭa, kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum kāṇpa eṉa iruvaraiyumē eḻuvāyāka, vaittuk kāṭṭum naṭaippāṭu aṟiyattakum. ip pālāṭciyaic caṅkap pulavarkaḷām pēricāttaṉārum ammūvaṉārum oppupa.
pālvarain tamaittal allatu avarvayiṉ
cālpaḷantaṟitaṟku yāa yārō			(pēricāttaṉār kuṟun 366)

piṟitoṉ ṟākak kūṟum
āṅkum ākkumō vāḻiya pālē		(ammūvaṉār ain. 10)
orē pāṭaliṉ āciriyar mōtācaṉār kātaṟ pāṅkai aḻunta ārāyntār. kātalarāvār vevvēṟiṭattavarkaḷāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatillai. muṉpiṉ mukamaṟiyāp putiyavarkaḷātal vēṇṭum eṉpatilla. nāḷtōṟum palkāl paḻakikkoṇṭu varu ōr iḷaiyāṉum iḷaiyāḷum eṉṟō orunāḷ tiṭīreṉak kātal tōṉṟap pārttal iyalpēyām. ciṟuvaṉ ciṟumiyāy irunta paruvattu ivviruvarum talaimuṭi paṟṟiyiḻuttu aṭittuk koṇṭaṉarē, ceviliyar iṭaimaṟittu vilakkavum viṭātu caṇṭaiyiṭṭaṉarē. iṉṟē iṇainta malarmālai pōla maṇa makkaḷāka viḷaṅkak kāṇkiṉṟōm nallūḻiṉ viḷaiyāṭṭaṉṟē itu eṉṟu talaivaṉ talaiviyaic ciṟuparuvam toṭṭu aṟintōr avviruvartam uṭaṉ pōkkaik kaṇṭu pālāṟṟalai viyakkiṉṟaṉar.
ivaṉivaḷ aimpāl paṟṟavum ivaḷivaṉ
puṉtalai ori vāṅkunaḷ pariyavum
kātaṟ ceviliyar tavirppavum tavirātu
ētil ciṟuceru uṟupa maṉṉō
nallaimaṉ ṟamma pālē melliyal
tuṇaimalarp piṇaiyal aṉṉavivar
maṇamakiḻ iyaṟkai kāṭṭi yōyē.				(kuṟun. 229)
mōtācaṉāriṉ ippāṭal “pālatāṇaiyiṉ” eṉṟa tol kāppiyattukku nalviḷakkamātal kāṇka. iḷampūraṇar tol kāppiyattukku mutaluraiyāciriyar. ivar urai uḷḷa uraikaḷuḷ ēṟṟamuṭaiyatu. oṉṟē vēṟē (1038) eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟku.
“oruvaraiyoruvar kaṇṭuḻiyellām puṇarccivēṭkai tōṟṟāmai yiṉ, pālatāṇaiyāṉ oruvaraiyoruvar puṇartaṟ kuṟippōṭu kāṇpa eṉṟavāṟu”
eṉa ariya viḷakkam taruvar iḷampūraṇar. kaṇṭatum kātalkaṇṭa mutaṟpārvaiyilē kātalkoṇṭu viṭutal eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭai orupāl maṟuppar. āṇ peṇ pālarkaḷaic cantikkac ceyvatu vitiyiṉ vēlaiyaṉṟu. avarkaḷ mutalmuṟai cantikkalām; palamuṟai cantittum irukkalām. ic cantip pellām pālatāṇaiyillai. mutalnāḷ mutaṟpārvai yilō, piṉṉāl pala pāriviyalō, oru pārvaiyaik kātalōṭu pārkkac ceyvatē vitiyiṉ āṇai eṉṟu teḷivippār uraiyāciriyar. ikkātaṟ puṇarcciviti kūṭṭiya mutaluṟavu iyaṟkaip puṇarcci eṉṟu potuvākap peyar peṟum. talaivaṉum talaiviyumām viti muṉṉarē uṇṭeṉiṉum tōṉṟātu, kālam pārttu aṭaṅkiyirukkum. kālamāvatu iruvarum kāmam nukarvataṟkuriya kumarap paruvam. apparuvam vāyātārkkuk kaṇṇil kātalnōkkam eḻātu; piṟar kaṇṇil eḻunta kātalnōkkattaip purintu koḷḷum aṟivum irātu. ātaliṉ aṭaṅkiyirunta viti, puṇartaṟkuriya iruvarum āḷāṉa piṉṉarttāṉ veḷippaṭuvatu muṟaiyum payaṉumām. itukarutiyē, tolkāppiyamum iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷum, kāmapuṇarcci eṉṟu immutaṟpuṇarccikkup peyariṭṭuḷa. ikkuṟiyīṭu iyaṟkaip puṇarcci eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭṭiṉum virumpattakkatu. ituvarai ārāynta kāmat toṭarpupaṟṟi iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ, tirukkōvaiyār uraiyāciriyarkaḷuma toṭaiviṭaipaṭa eḻutiyuḷḷaṉar.
uḷḷappuṇarcciyum meyyuṟupuṇarcciyum
āṇpeṇ uṟavukkuk kātal kāraṇam eṉṟōm. kātalukkup pāl kāraṇam eṉṟōm. aṟitōṟum aṟiyāmai kaṇṭatallatu eṉṉa viḷakkam uṇṭōm? pālāvatu yātu? pālatāṇai eṉpatu eṉ? pāl kūṭṭu vittaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa? otta talaivaṉum talaiviyum pālatāṇaiyāl kātaṟ kuṟippoṭu kāṇpar eṉiṉ, avviruvarum ottavarkaḷāka iruppataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa? otta talaivaṉum talaiviyum pālatāṇaiyāl kātaṟ kuṟippoṭu kāṇpar eṉiṉ, avviruvarum ottavarkaḷāka iruppataṟkuk kāraṇam piṟituṇṭō? oppu eṉpatu etu? aḵtu orupiṟaviyoppā? palpiṟavi yoppā?
immai māṟi maṟumai yāyiṉum
nīyā kireṉ kaṇavaṉai
yāṉā kiyarniṉ ṉeñnēr pavaḷē.				(kuṟun.49)

niṉakkivaṉ makaṉāt tōṉṟi yatūum
maṉakkiṉi yāṟkunī makaḷā yatūum
paṇṭuma paṇṭum palpiṟap puḷavāl
kaṇṭa piṟaviyē yalla kārikai					(maṇimē. 24, 569)
eṉṟu ilakkiyam kūṟumāṅku, talaivaṉ talaiviyar vaḻivaḻik kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyarā? iṉṉa aiyaṅkaḷ nam aṟiviyal kiḷaikkiṉṟaṉa. tirukkōvai uraip pērāciriyar kātal vāḻkkaiyil vitikku uyariya matippu nalkavillai. pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam tōḻiyaṟ kūṭṭaṅkaḷukkup pāṅkaṉum tōḻiyum tuṇaiyāvār attaṇai yallatu talaivaṉ talaivi kātalukkuk kāraṇamākār aṉṟē; atupōla iyaṟkaippuṇarcciyil viti kūṭṭuvitta tuṇaiyallatu avartam aṉpukku kāraṇamākātu eṉpatu avvuraiyāciriyar tuṇipu. niṟka,
kātalartam maṉavoṟṟumaiyē akat tiṇaiyiṉ uyirp paṇpu. iv voṟṟumaiyai uḷḷap puṇarcci’ eṉṟu akavilakkaṇam kūṟum. evvakai akattiṇaip pāṭalukkum akattuṟaip pāṭalukkum uḷḷap puṇarcci iṉṟiyamaiyātatu; puṟa naṭaiyillātatu; iṟaimai cāṉṟatu. akappulavar ivvaṭippaṭaikku ūṟupaṭayātum kūṟār. ūṟupaṭā nilaiyil yātum kūṟuvar. ip pēruṇmai ivvārāycci muḻuvatum nīḷa niṉaiya vēṇṭuvatu eṉṟu kuṟikkoṇmiṉ.
āḷāṉa talaivaṉ talaivi uḷḷam puṇarntapiṉ uṭaṟpuṇarccikku avāvutal muṟaiyē. maṉam oṉṟiya kātalōr uṭal oṉṟutaṟkut takka taṉiyiṭam tērvar. iṭam vāyppiṉ iṉpappāyal koḷḷap piṉ vāṅkār. peṟṟōraik kalantu mēlceyvaṉa ceyvōm  eṉṉum aṟivu oṭār. maṇ cuḻaliṉum kaṭal poṅkiṉum malai peyariṉum viṇ vīḻiṉum aracu vitippiṉum makkaḷ etirppiṉum, varuvatu varuka eṉṟu, accam aṟiyār. kātal kātal kātal, kātal nīṅkiṟ cātal cātal cātal eṉṉum pērāṇmai koṇṭa iḷaiya uḷḷaṅkaḷ peṟṟōrkku añciyō piṟa accaṅkoṇṭō puṇarcciyait taḷḷippōṭa eṇṇā. iṭam vāyāmaiyiṉ puṇarcci nikaḻātirukkalām. iṭam vāyttum tāytuñcāmai nāytuñcāmai mutalāṉa iṭaiyūṟukaḷāl puṇarcci taṭaippaṭṭirukkalām. uḷḷam kalanta ciṉṉāṭkaḷil tirumaṇam naṭaipeṟṟirukkalām. kaḷavukkālattu uṭaṟ kūṭṭuṟavē illai eṉṟum, atu kūṭātu eṉṟum kūṟuvār koḷkai piḻaiyām; iyaṟkai yuṇarccikku muraṇām. kaṟpu eṉappaṭuvatu kātalartam meyttoṭarpukkup piṉ varum oḻukkamaṉṟu. oruvarai yoruvar uḷḷattāl niṉaitta appoḻutē kaṟpeṉṉum tiṇmai vēṇṭappaṭum. meyppuṇarvu paṭṭapiṉ, peṟṟōr icaiyārāyiṉ, eṉceyvatu? kaṟpuk keṭumaṉṟē; aṉṉōr icaivu teriyumvarai uḷḷam puṇarnta aḷavil vaittuk koḷvōm eṉṟa pakuttaṟivu uṇmaik kaṟputtiṟamāmō? “uḷḷattāṉ vēṭkai celliṉum meyyuṟṟārāyiṟṟu” eṉa aṟutiyiṭṭuraippar iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ uraiyāciriyar. ātaliṉ kaṟpuk karuti, kaḷavil meyyuṟār eṉpatu aṟamākātu. kaḷavuk kālattu meyyiṉpam tuyttamai paṟṟiya akappāṭalkaḷ palavuḷa. meyyuṟum ceykai iṉṟāyiṉa, iravukkuṟi mutalāṉa ettuṇaiyō tuṟaikaḷ ciṟanta payaṉilavāy oḻiyuṅkāṇ.
ayattuvaḷar paiñcāy muruntiṉ aṉṉa
nakaippolin tilaṅkum eyiṟukeḻu tuvarvāy
ākat tarumpiya mulaiyaḷ paṇaittōḷ
māttāṭ kuvaḷai malarpiṇait taṉṉa
māyitaḻ maḻaikkaṇ māa yōḷoṭu
pēyum aṟiyā maṟaiyamai puṇarcci	(akam. 62)
talaivaṉ mutaṟpuṇarcciyai mīṇṭum niṉaiyum pāṭṭu itu. māniṟat talaiviyiṉ cevvitaḻ cuvaittu, arumpiya mārpaiyum akaṉṟa tōḷaiyum taḻuvi nukarnta maṟaittaṟkariya mutaṟ kūṭṭuṟavai niṉaivu koḷkiṉṟāṉ. pēyum naṭamāṭṭam oṭuṅkit tūṅkum naḷḷiravil tuytta kaḷavup puṇarcci eṉṟu perumitaṅ koḷkiṉṟāṉ. uḷḷap puṇarccikku maṟaiviṭam vēṇṭiya tillai. pēyum aṟiyā naṭuyāmam vēṇṭiyatillai. ātaliṉ paraṇar ippāṭṭil kaḷavuk kātalariṉ meymmuyakkattaik kūṟukiṟār eṉpatu veḷippaṭai muṉpiṉ yātoru uṟavum mukavaṟivum illātār uḷḷam aṉpuṟṟu uṭal iṉpuṟṟa kāṭciyai orukuṟuntokaip pāṭṭāl aṟikiṉṟōm. kātalaṉ puṇarcci niṟaivil kātaliyai nōkki, ippōtu nam uṭalkaḷā kalantaṉa? “aṉpuṭai neñcan tām kalantaṉavē” (kuṟun 40) eṉṟu nākarikamāka nalam pārāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ.
2. iṭantalaippāṭu
taṟceyalāka orunāḷ kaṇṭa iruvar uḷḷam puṇarntaṉar: kātalarāyiṉar. aṭaṅkiya vēṭkai perukiṟṟu. mutalnāḷ kaṇṭaviṭattē iṉṟum kāṇalām eṉṟa nampikkaiyōṭu talaivaṉ tāṉē avviṭam celkiṉṟāṉ. ārāk kātaluṭaiya talaiviyum avaṉukku muṉṉarē vantu aṅku niṟkiṉṟāḷ. ēṉ? orumuṟai āṇ pulliyapiṉ, peṇṇukkuk kāmam ciṟakkum. muṉpōla tōḻiyarōṭu viḷaiyāṭa vantum viḷaiyāṭṭil maṉaṅkoḷḷāḷ pūppaṟittu malar toṭukka uḷḷam nāṭāḷ. avaḷ kātaṟ perukkai uṇarnta talaivaṉ, “āṭalum toṭuttalum ceyyātu taṉittuniṟkum nī yārō: uṉakku eṉ vaṇakkam. kaṇṭavar kaṇṇaip paṟikkum aḻakiyē! kaṭal vāḻ teyvamō nī?”
yārai yōniṟ ṟoḻutaṉam viṉavutum
kaṇṭōr taṇṭā nalattai teṇṭiraip
peruṅkaṭaṟ parappiṉamarntuṟai aṇaṅkō	(naṟ. 155)
eṉṟu taṉ kātaṟpaṟaiyai muḻakkukiṉṟāṉ; aḻakut teyvamākat toḻukiṉṟāṉ. ‘vaḷai olippa naṇṭiṉai āṭṭupavaḷē! naṇṭiṉai āṭṭupavaḷ pōlak kūntalāl mukattai maṟaittu niṟpavaḷē mālai maṟaiyiṉ niṉmuyakkam kiṭaikkum” (aiṅ. 197) eṉṟu vāyūṟi moḻikiṉṟāṉ. eṉ collukkellām maṟumoḻi collātu nāṇupavaḷē! niṉ nāṇam eṉ kāmattaip perukkiṉ tāṅkuvatu eppaṭi? (“kāmam kaimmikiṉ tāṅkutal eḷitō”) eṉṟu tāṉē tāṉē paṉṉukiṟāṉ.
iṭantalaippāṭu paṟṟi mūṉṟu pāṭalkaḷē (aiṅ. 197. naṟ. 39; 153) caṅkavilakkiyattil uḷa. puṇarcci vēṭkai kāṇappaṭuvataṉṟip puṇarnta kuṟippuc ciṟitu ippāṭalkaḷil illai. iyaṟkaip puṇarcci, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam, tōḻiyiṟkūṭṭam eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ kātalariṭaip puṇarcciyuṇṭu eṉpatai veḷippaṭaiyākak kāṭṭuvatu pōla, iṭantalaippāṭu (tol. 1443) eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭu kāṭṭavillai. itaṉ uṭkōḷ eṉṉa? kaṇṭāl muyaṅkutal, muyaṅkutaṟkē kāṇutal eṉpatu ācaikkāmam ākumaṉṟi aṉpuk kāmam ākātu. kātalarkaḷ puṇarvu viruppiṉṟiyum kaṇṭu koḷvār; kāṇuntōṟum puṇarvu mēṟkoḷḷār eṉpatu (kīḻāṉa paruva eḻuccikku iṭaṅkoṭā) uyarnta kātalākum. iyaṟkaip puṇarcciyil kaṇṭu kalanta talaivaṉukakum talaivikkum avvaḷavil maṉaniṟaivu ēṟpaṭumā? pirinta piṉ puṇarnta niṉaivum puṇarntār uruvamum neñcai arittaṉa. putiya periya uṟavai mīṇṭum orumuṟai kaḷittuk kāṇpatē iṭantalaippāṭu. it talaippāṭṭāl ārā iḷaiya neñcaṅkaḷ vēkantaṇikiṉṟaṉa; amaitikku varukiṉṟaṉa; nampikkai koḷkiṉṟaṉa.
3. pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam
iṭantalaip pāṭṭiṉ piṉṉar, naṇpaṉ talaivaṉaik kāṉkiṉṟāṉ; mukaccōrvaik kaṇṭu iravellām uṟakka millaiyō eṉṟupotuppaṭak kēṭkiṉṟāṉ. kāmattaippaṟṟi veḷippaṭaiyāka uraiyāṭal āṇ naṇparkaḷiṉ maṉakkūṟu. uṟaṅkavillaiyō eṉṟa viṉāvukku oru kuṟacciṟumiyiṉ taṇṇirpōlum meṉcāyal tīp pōlum eṉ neñcurattai avittuviṭṭatu;
ciṟukuṭik kuṟavaṉ neruntōṭ kuṟumakaḷ
nīrō raṉṉa cāyal
tiyō raṉṉaveṉ uraṉavit taṉṟē.	(kuṟun 95)
eṉṟu talaivaṉ oḷivu maṟaiviṉṟip perumitam tōṉṟa maṟumoḻi kiṉṟāṉ. ivvoḻukkam niṉ aṟivukkum perumaikkum kuṭimaikkum takumō eṉṟu iṭikkum naṇpaṉukku, `ivaiyellām  uṉṉaiviṭa eṉakkutteriyum. avaḷ kuvaḷaik kaṇṇaip pārppataṟkumuṉ pēca vēṇṭiya pēccukkaḷ’ eṉat talaivaṉ etiruraikkiṉṟāṉ. (naṟ. 190) aṟivunilaiyil niṟkum iḷampāṅkaṉ. “talaivi ariyavaḷ; avaḷ niṉaippai viṭṭoḻi (naṟ. 201) eṉṟu mēlum taṭutturaippavē. talaivaṉ āṟṟalaṉ āyiṉāṉ. kāmakkiṟukkaṉāyk kātaṟpittaṉāy kaikaṭantu pēcal āṉāṉ.
kēḷir vāḻiyō kēḷir nāḷumeṉ
neñcupiṇik koṇṭa añcil ōtip
peruntōṭ kuṟumakaḷ ciṟumel lākam
orunāṭ puṇarap puṇariṉ
araināḷ vāḻkkaiyum vēṇṭaleṉ yāṉē	(kuṟun 280)
iṟaivaṉaip pēcāta nāḷellām piṟavāta nāḷāka aṉpaṉ karutuvāṉ. pōrkkaḷam pukkup puṇpaṭāta nāḷellām puṟaṅkoṭutta nāḷāka maṟavaṉ karutuvāṉ. akam pukuntavaḷiṉ melliya ākattai mēvāta nāḷellām vāḻā nāḷākak kātalaṉ karutuvāṉ.
makkaḷ āṇṭu palavāka āyiram āyiram nāṭkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. palar vāḻkkaiyai niṉaintu pārppiṉ, ellā nāḷum ciṟappiṉṟi, orunāḷ pōlavē kaḻikiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉanilaiyai mēṟkāṭṭi kuṟuntokait talaivaṉ veṟukkiṟāṉ. ituvarai vāḻntatai vāḻkakaiiyāka avaṉ matikkavili. iṉiyum ivvāṟu celvatai avaṉ virumpavillai. niṉaiyattakka viṉaicciṟappuṭaiya oru nāḷāvatu vēṇṭum eṉṟum, annāḷ taṉ uḷḷam kavarnta kaḷviyiṉ ākattait taḻuvum nāḷē eṉṟum, ataṉpiṉ vīṇvāḻkkai ciṟupoḻutum vēṇṭām  eṉṟum, ārāk kātalāl taṉ naṇpaṉ atirum kuṟikkōḷaic collukiṟāṉ. kēḷir kēḷir eṉṟu tōḻaṉai aṉputōṉṟa aṭuttaṭuttu viḷittal kāṇka. uṇmai naṭpu maṟuppaṟiyātu; kāmattukkuk kaṇṇillai (kuṟun 78); annōy paṟṟiya talaivaṉukku eppōtum uṟakkamillai (aiṅ 173); eṉceyvatu eṉṟu iyalpu uṇarnta tōḻaṉ talaivaṉ coṉṉa kuṟiyiṭam celkiṉṟāṉ. muṉṉarē vantuniṟkum talaiviyiṉ aṉpaik kaṇṭu viyakkiṉṟāṉ (aiṅ 174) pāṅkaṉāl talaivaṉukkut talaiviyōṭu oru kūṭṭam nikaḻkiṉṟatu. itu pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam paṟṟi 27 akappāṭalkaḷ uḷa. ivaṟṟuḷ pāṅkaṉ kūṟṟāy varuvaṉa iraṇṭē (kuṟun. 78,204) eñciya irupattaintum talaivaṉ kūṟṟāvaṉa. caṅkappulavarkaḷuḷ miḷaip peruṅ kantaṉār eṉpavarē talaivaṉ kūṟṟākavum pāṅkaṉ kūṟṟākavum iru pāṭalkaḷ poruḷ toṭarcci paṭap pāṭiyirukkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. caṅkap pāṭal ellām oṉṟōṭoṉṟu karuttuk kōvaiyillāt taṉittaṉit tuṟaippāṭalkaḷē eṉiṉum kantaṉār pōlum ōriru pulavarkaḷ cila tuṟait toṭar amaiyap pāṭiya kōvaip pāṭalkaḷum uḷa eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakum.
miḷaipperuṅ kantaṉā:
kāmam kāmam eṉpa kāmam
aṇaṅkum piṇiyum aṉṟē nuṇaṅkik
kaṭuttalum taṇitalum iṉṟē yāṉai
kuḷakumeṉ ṟāṇmatam pōlap
pāṇiyum uṭaittatu kāṇunarp peṟiṉē	(kuṟun. 136)
itu talaimakaṉ pāṅkaṟku uraittatu.
kāmam kāmam eṉpa kāmam
aṇaṅkum piṇiyum aṉṟē niṉaippiṉ
mutaiccuvaṟ kalitta muṟṟā iḷampul
mūtā taivan tāṅku
viruntē kāmam peruntōḷōyē	(kuṟun 204)
itu talaimakaṟkup pāṅkaṉ uraittatu.
irupāṭṭilum mutalaṭikaḷ iraṇṭum ottaṉa. kāmam eṉṉum col paṇṭu iṭattiṟkēṟpa uyarkāmattaiyum iḻikāmattaiyumkuṟittatu pōlum, uyarnta kāmattai iḻivāṉa kāmamāka ulakōr karutuvar; av vuyarkāmam iḻikāmam pōla varuttamum nōyum taruvatillai, eṉṟu miḷaipperuṅ kantaṉār, kātalai nukarnta talaivaṉ vāyilākavum, avaṉai iṭitturaittam aṟiñaṉ vāyilākavum ulakōrkku maṟuppu uraippar; kāmattiṉ ciṟappaip pulappaṭuttuvar. ivvuyar kāmaḻāvatu yātu; aṉpuṭaiya etirpālāraik kāṇumpōtu veḷippaṭuvatu eṉkiṉṟāṉ talaivaṉ. iḵtu ivaṉ tuyttuk kaṇṭatu. kāmamāvatu oru maṉanilai; virumpiṉōrai atu, viṭātu veṟuttōrai atu toṭātu eṉṟu iṭitturaikkiṉṟāṉ pāṅkaṉ. iḵtu ivaṉ tuyppaṉmaiyāl vanta aṟivurai; allatu, kaṭamai yuṇarcciyāl eḻunta urai eṉṟum koḷḷalām pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭac ceyyuṭkaḷ ellāmē aṟivuc ceṟivum ilakkiyap polivum uṭaiyaṉa. kātaliyar tam kaṇ eṉṉum piṉṉā valaiyuḷḷum, kūntal eṉṉum piṉṉiya valaiyuḷḷum paṭṭa āṭavar tam neñcat tuṭippaik kāṭṭuvaṉa. ip pāṭalkaḷāl kātalāvatu oṟṟumai vēṟṟumaittaram pārttu ārāycci ceytu aṟivunilaiyil nikaḻvataṉṟu; `itaṟkitu māṇṭatu eṉṉātu ataṟpaṭṭu āṇṭoḻintaṉṟē māṇtakai neñcam’ (kuṟun. 184) eṉṟu oru talaivaṉ kūṟiyapaṭi, kāraṇam kaṭanta tūya maṉanilaiyil amaivatu eṉṟa uṇmaiyaik kaṟkiṉṟōm
aintiṇai ilakkiya māntarkaḷil 75 paṭaippu iruvakaip payaṉuṭaiyatu. cekappiriyar eḻutiya `paṉṉiraṇṭām iravu’ nāṭakattil, ilīriyāk kōmakaṉ ārciṉō celvap perumāṭṭi oluviyāvaik kātalikkiṉṟāṉ. ivaṉ kātalukku avaḷ iṇakkakkuṟippu nalkavillai. ātaliṉ taṉ pāṅkaṉ cecāriyōvait tūtu viṭukkavē avaṉ olīviyāvai nēriṟ kaṇṭu avaḷ taṉiyaḻakaiyum ārciṉōviṉ peruṅkātalaiyum kaviñaṉ pōl eṭuttiyampukiṉṟāṉ. mika nalam peṟṟa nī oru tamiyaḷāy mūppaṭaiyalāmā? uṉ ellaiyaṟṟa aḻakukku ulakil orupaṭi vēṇṭāmā? eṉṟu iṉikkap pēcukiṉṟāṉ. iṅṅṉ.
talaiviyāvāḷaip pāṅkaṉ nērniṉṟu kāṇalum muṉṉiṉṟu moḻitalum tamiḻ akattiṇaikku ovvā; tamiḻakam peṟṟa makaḷir nāṇattukku ēlā īṇṭu pāṅkaṉ talaiviyait tāṉkāṇpāṉ, avaḷāl kāṇappaṭāṉ. talaivi pāṅkaṉ taṉpakkamākac celvataik kaṇṭāluṅkūṭa, avaṉai oru vaḻippōkkaṉ eṉavē karutiviṭuvāḷ. taṉṉaip pārttuvarat talaivaṉ viṭutta tōḻaṉ eṉa eṇṇāḷ ēṉ? talaivaṉatu tōḻarkaḷ yār eṉpatupaṟṟi avaḷukkut teriyātu. `tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam pōlappāṅkaṉ uraiyāṭi iṭainiṉṟu kūṭṭāmaiyiṉ’ (tol. 1047) eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, “taṉ vayaṟpāṅkaṉ avaḷvayiṟ pāṅku ceyyāṉ” (tol. 1443) eṉap pērāciriyarum pāṅkaṉ karumattai varaiyaṟuttal kāṇka.
- mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkam 
putiya pārvai cūṉ 2016


akattiṇai pākupāṭu
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam
kuṟitta iṭattut talaivi vantu niṟkiṉṟāḷā eṉpataip pārttuc collum ōr aṟivippāḷaṉē pāṅkaṉāvāṉ. eṉiṉum, ivaṉ toṭarpāl akat talaivaṉukku irunalam uṇṭu. iṉpa veḷḷattāl, tuṉpap puyalāl neñcu alaiyumpōtu, avaṟṟaip piṟariṭam pēciṉāl āṟutal uṇṭākum. kēṭpataṟku uṟṟa naṇparkaḷum naṇpikaḷum vēṇṭum talaivaṉ etirpārā nilaiyil oru naṅkai pāl kātal koṇṭu taṉ neñcakattaip putiya uṇarccikaḷukkup paṟikoṭuttu viṭṭāṉ. kāma vaṇuvum kāma curamum avaṉ uḷḷattait tiṉṟaṉa.
ñāyiṟu kāyum vevvaṟai maruṅkiṟ
kaiyil ūmaṉ kaṇṇiṟ kākkuma
veṇṇey uṇaṅkal pōlap
parantaṉ ṟinnōy nōṉṟukoḷaṟ karitē	(kuṟun 58)
eṉṟapaṭi, kaḷavuttalaivaṉ taṉvayam iḻantu, kāmavayattaṉ āṉāṉ.
tirumaṇattukkuppiṉ talaivi uṭaṉvāḻvāḷātaliṉ, talaivaṉatu kāmavēkattukku iṭaṉillai. kaḷavoḻukkattō avaḷ kāṇaṟkum kalattaṟkum ariyavaḷ. ataṉāl avaṉukku mēlum kāmacuram perukiṉ uyirpōtalum kūṭum: aṟivu tiriyavum ceyyum. innilaiyil pāṅkaṉ oru paṟṟukkōṭu. avaṉiṭattut taṉ talaiviyait `teyvaccāyal uṭaiyavaḷ, melliyaḷ’ arumpiya mulaiyaḷ, civanta vāyiṉaḷ (aiṅ 255) eṉṟu collun tōṟum ciṟupāmpukkuṭṭi kāṭṭāṉaiyai varuttiyatupōla avaḷ melluṟuppukkaḷ eṉṉaittākkiṉa. (kuṟun 119) eṉṟu colluntōṟum talaivaṉukku maṉavēkam taṇiyavum, aṟivu nilaikku varavum kāṇkiṉṟōm. talaivaṉ kūṟṟāka 25 pāṭal iruppataṟku ivvuḷaviyalē kāraṇamām.
pāṅkaṉ talaivaṉuṭaṉ cērntu avaṉ celluḻi ellām cellum vaḻakkamuṭaiyavaṉ. viṭṭup piriyāta tōḻamai koṇṭavaṉ. talaivaṉ oruttipāṟ paṭṭa piṉṉum pāṅkaṉ muṉpōl uṭaṉvarutul takātaṉṟō. itaṉai avaṉ eppaṭi aṟivāṉ? talaivaṉatu putiya uṇarcciyaiyum putiya uṟavaiyum avaṉtaṉ meyppāṭṭālum collālum terintu koṇṭa pāṅkaṉ kuṇamum kulamum aṟivum cuṭṭi māṟṟa muyalkiṉṟāṉ; payaṉiṉmai kaṇṭapiṉ avaṉōṭu kūṭa varuvatai niṟuttik koḷkiṉṟāṉ. itaṉāl talaivaṉukkut talaiviyaik kāṇavēṇṭumpōtu taṉittuc cellum peruvāyppuk kiṭaikkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu pāṅkaṉ talaivaṉatu kāmapputumaiyaik kātu koṭuttuk kēṭkum ceviyaṉāy, mēlum ciṟitu iṭaiyūṟum ceyyāta tōḻaṉāyk kaḷaviṉ toṭakkattē vantu pōyviṭak kāṇkiṉṟōm. aintiṇai ilakkiyattu ivaṉvaravu itaṉoṭu muṟṟum.
mēl viḷaṅkiya iyaṟkaip puṇarcci, iṭantalaippāṭu, pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam eṉṟa mūṉṟu kāṭcikkum kuṟiyiṭam oṉṟē ākavum, talaivaṉ iṭantalaippāṭupōl mīṇṭum ēṉ tāṉē ceṉṟiruttal kūṭātu? pāṅkaṉai viṭuttataṉ nōkkam eṉṉa? pal iḻanta kiḻappacu iḷampullait taṭavi makiḻnta maṉaviṉpamē kāmaviṉpam; kāmamāvatu taṉṉai uṇarntār uṇarātār eṉap pārātu ellāraiyum paṟṟum perum pētaimaiyuṭaiyatu; iṉi uḷḷal kūṭātu eṉṟellām kātalkāyum talaivaṉaip pāṅkaṉ nakaiyāṭi iṭitturaikkiṉṟaṉaṉ. taṉ vēṭkai takkatē eṉṟum, iṭippuraiyāl oḻiyātatu eṉṟum uṇartta virumapiṉāṉ talaivaṉ. pāṅkaṉ taṉ collai nampāṉ; taṉṉai mayakkiyavaḷaik kaṇṭāl nampuvāṉ; piṉṉar iṭitturaiyāṉ eṉa eṇṇiṉāṉ.
amma vāḻi kēḷir muṉṉiṉṟu
kaṇṭaṉi rāyiṟ kaḻaṟalir maṉṉō...
naṟṟēr vaḻuti koṟkai muṉṟuṟai
vaṇṭuvāy tiṟanta vāṅkukaḻi neytaṟ
pōtupuṟaṅ koṭutta uṇkaṇ
mātar vāṇmukam mataiiya nōkkē.	(akam. 130)
`tōḻa! eṉṉaip piṇittavaḷiṉ nōkku matar matarppu uṭaiyatu. avaḷ kaṇ pacumaikkuk kaipaṭāta, vaṇṭuvāy tiṟanta, tuṟaiyiṭattu vaḷarnta neytaṟpūvum nikarākātē. in nōkkaḻakiyai muṉṉiṉṟu nī orumuṟai kaṇṭuviṭṭāṟ pōtum; piṉ eṉṉaik kaṭinturaiyāy eṉṟu ēvavē, pāṅkaṉuma talaivaṉ coṉṉa kuṟiyiṭam ceṉṟu, attakaiyāḷaik kāṇkiṉṟāṉ. `neytaṟpū maṇakkum kūntalaḻakiyiṉ piṭip paṭṭōr iravum uṟaṅkuvārō? pāmpu kaṭittaṉṉa pāṭupaṭārō?’ eṉṟu pāṅkaṉē uṇarak kāṇkiṉṟōm.
iravi ṉāṉum iṉtuyil aṟiyātu
aravuṟu tuyaram eytupa toṇṭit
taṇṇāṟu neytaliṉ nāṟum
piṉṉirum kūntal aṇaṅkuṟ ṟōrē.	(aiṅ. 173)
4. tōḻiyiṟ puṇarcci
caṅka ilakkiyattu 882 kaḷavup pāṭalkaḷ uḷa. ivaṟṟuḷ 40 pāṭalkaḷē iyaṟkaip puṇarcci, iṭantalaippāṭu. pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉṟa mūṉṟu vakaikkum uriyaṉa. eñciya 842 pāṭal - 95 viḻukkāṭu - tōḻiyiṟ puṇarcci eṉṉum oru vakaikkē varuvaṉa. itaṉāl tōḻiyiṉṟik kātalarkaḷiṉ kaḷavoḻukkam nīḷātu eṉpatum, aintiṇai ilakkiyap paṭaippukkut tōḻi eṉṉum āḷ (pāttiram) iṉṟiyamaiyātavaḷ eṉpatum, tōḻiyiṟ puṇarccikkuriya tuṟaikaḷē pulavarkaḷiṉ neñcaik kavarntaṉa eṉpatum peṟalām. tōḻiyiṉ toṭarpukkup piṉnikaḻum kātaṟ ceytikaḷ aḷavila. avaṟṟaiyellām iṅkuk kūṟap pukutal mikaiyum kuṟaiyumām, vēṇṭumaḷavē viḷampuval.
kātaliyait taṟceyalāka evvaḷavu nāḷ kāṇa muṭiyum. pāṅkaṉ evvaḷavu utavi ceyvāṉ? niṉaitta viṭamellām cuṟṟittiriyum pōkkurimai āṭavarkkut tamiḻc camutāyattukku uṇṭu. aṉṉa urimai kulamakaḷirkkum kumariyarkkum ili.
yāyē kaṇṇiṉum kaṭuṅkā talaḷē
entaiyum nilaṉuṟap poṟāaṉ cīṟaṭi civappa
evaṉila kuṟumakaḷ iyaṅkuti eṉṉum	(akam. 12)

kāppum pūṭṭiciṉ kaṭaiyum pōkalai
pētai yallai mētaiyaṅ kuṟumakaḷ	(akam. 7)
iravum pakalum tāy tantaiyiṉ kaṭuṅkāppukku illak kumariyarai uḷḷākkuvatu tamiḻ nākarikam. “vaṇṇac cīṟaṭi maṇmakaḷ aṟintilaḷ” eṉac cilappatikāramum, “kaṉṉik kāvalum kaṭiyiṟ kāvalum vilaimakaḷirkku illai” eṉa maṇimēkalaik kāppiyamum moḻital kāṇka. orukāl peṟṟōr icaivupeṟṟu veḷic celliṉum viṭātu tōḻiyum uṭaṉ celvāḷ.
yāmē piriviṉ ṟiyainta tuvarā naṭpiṉ
irutalaip puḷḷiṉ oruyi rammē	(akam. 12)
eṉat talaivi tōḻiyiṉ iṇaipiriyāk kēṇmaiyaik kapilar ilporuḷuvamai eṉṉum kaṟpaṉaiyāl veḷippaṭuttuvar. ivviruvarum orunāḷ nīrviḷaiyāṭap pārtta talaimakaṉ avhtam kaṭunaṭpait teḷintāṉ. teppattiṉ talaip pākattait tōḻi piṭittāl, ivaḷum appākattaiyē piṭikkiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ ataṉ kaṭaippakutiyait taḻuviṉāl ivaḷum ataṉaiyē taḻuva muntukiṉṟāḷ. tōḻi teppappiṭi vaḻuvi nīruḷ mūḻkiṉāl, ivaḷum uṭaṉ mūḻkuvāḷ pōlum (kuṟun 222)” eṉak kaṇ cāṉṟākat talaivaṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭaṉaṉ. tōḻi nīṅkā ōr iṭaiyūṟu eṉṟum, tōḻiyiṉ tōḻamaiyaip peṟṟālaṉṟit talaiviyiṉ tōḷaip peṟutal aritu eṉṟum avaṉukkup pulaṉāyiṟṟu.
kaḷavut toṭarpait tōḻikku uṇarttuvatu eṅṅaṉ? talaivi nāṇoṭu piṟantu nāṇilē vaḷarntavaḷ. ātaliṉ tōḻiyiṭattut taṉ putiya uṟavaik kūṟavē kūṟāḷ. kūṟum poṟuppō kūcāp piṟappiṉāṉākiya āṭavaṉaic cārntatu. tōḻiyum talaiviyum oruṅku irukkumpōtu ceṉṟu veḷip paṭaiyākavō kuṟippākavō kaḷavai nayamākap pulappaṭuttal talaivaṉ muṟaiyākum.

oṉṟu, irappāṉpōl eḷivantu collum ulakam
purappāṉ pōlvatōr matukaiyum uṭaiyaṉ
vallārai vaḻipaṭ ṭoṉṟaṟin tāṉpōl
nallārkaṭ ṭōṉṟum aṭakkamum uṭaiyaṉ
illōṉ puṉkaṇ īkaiyiṟ ṟaṇikka
vallāṉ pēlvatōr vaṉmaiyum uṭaiyaṉ
aṉṉāṉ oruvaṉtaṉ āṇṭakai viṭṭeṉṉaic
colluñcoṟ kēṭṭī cuṭariḻāy	(kali. 47)
eḷimai, perumitam, aṟivu aṭakkam, aruḷ āṟṟla uṭaiya oru nampi evvaṉmaiyum illāta eṉpāl vantāṉ; niṉṉai iṉṟi eṉakku vāḻkkai illai eṉṟāṉ; oru peṇporuṭṭut taṉ āṇ takutikaḷaiyellām kai viṭṭāṉ. ivaṉ col nampattakkatō? taṉi oru peṇṇāl ārāyntu uṇmai kāṇum takutiyuṭaiyatō eṉa ivvāṟu talaivaṉiṉ tōṟṟattilum paṇpilum tōḻi īṭupaṭṭāḷ. avaṉ kuṟaiyai muṭittu vaikkavum eṇṇiṉāḷ. eṉiṉum talaiviyai aṇukuvatu eppaṭi? kaḷavuc ceyti tōḻikkut teriyum eṉṟu aṟintāṟkūṭa avaḷ nāṇam poṟukkātē? meyyāṉa nikaḻcciyaic colliyō (kali. 60), poyyāka nikaḻcciyaik kaṟpittō (kali. 37, akam. 32) iru poruḷpaṭumpaṭi toṭaramaittu, evvakaiyāṉum talaiviyiṉ melliya nāṇam ūṟupaṭātavāṟu, avaḷ neñcattai aṟiya muyalka eṉṟu tolkāppiyar vaḻi kūṟuvar.
meyyiṉum poyyiṉum vaḻinilai piḻaiyātu
palvēṟu kavarporuḷ nāṭṭat tāṉum	(tol. 1059)
makaḷiṟku nāṇuṇarcci mikak kūriyatu. oru peṇṇiṉ kātaluḷḷattai neruṅkip paḻakum maṟṟoru tōḻip peṇ viṉavattuṇiyāḷ. “eṉ tōḻi naṉi nāṇuṭaiyaḷ (aiṅ. 205) eṉṟu taṉ talaiviyiṉ iyalpai oru tōḻiyē kūṟutal kāṇka. tōḻi eṉṉa, īṉṟu vaḷarttat tāykūṭat taṉ oru makaḷiṉ kātaṟkaravaik kēṭka añcuvāḷeṉṟāl tirututal nallavar nāṇiṉ nuṇmai aṟiyattakkatō? `taṉmakaḷ pōkkait tāyē aṟintukoḷḷaṭṭum; itu paṟṟi namakkeṉṉa kavalai eṉṟu vāḷā irātu, vāy koḻutta ūrp peṇṭukaḷ orunāḷ irunāḷ alla, palanāḷ vantu vantu eṉṉiṭam eṉmakaḷ paṟṟic collukiṟārkaḷ. yāṉō avaḷiṭam atupaṟṟi oṉṟum vāytiṟappatillai. ētum kēṭkap pukiṉ, nāṇam avaḷaik kollum. ātaliṉ ūrār collum ceytiyai mikavum maṟaittu vaittuk koḷkiṟēṉ”.
uvakkuna ḷāyiṉum uṭaluna ḷāyiṉum
yāyaṟin tuṇara eṉṉār tīvāy
alarviṉai mēval ampaṟ peṇṭir
iṉṉaḷ iṉaiyaḷniṉ makaḷeṉap paṉṉāḷ
eṉakkuvan turaippavum taṉakkuraip paṟiyēṉ
nāṇuvaḷ ivaḷeṉa naṉikaran turaiyum yāṉ	(akam. 203)
eṉa oru tāy kūṟṟil vaittuk kapilar peṇpāliṉ nāṇac cevviyaip pulaṉākkuvar. kumariyar tam kāmakkuṟippaic collālum veḷippaṭuttuvatillai. eṇṇattālum aṟiya iṭaṅkoṭuppatillai. ākaliṉ, aṉṉavar akamaṉattaik kāṇpataṟku oruvaḻi pōtāteṉṟu tōḻikku eḻuvaḻi kūṟuvar tolkāppiyar. avaiyāvaṉa. 1. putiya maṇam 2. putiya kaḷai 3. putiya oḻukkam 4. uṇavil maṉam cellāmai 5. ceyalai maṟaittal 6. taṉiyē cellal 7. taṉiyē iruttal.
maṭalēṟutal
talaiviyiṉ naṟkuṟippu aṟintapiṉ, kātalarkaḷ pakalum iravum kāṇavum kūṭavum tōḻi vaḻiceyvāḷ. iyaṟkaip puṇarcci mutalāṉa muṉṉaik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ nikaḻnta iṭattē nikaḻum. tōḻi kūṭṭuvikkum kūṭṭaṅkaḷō vēṟuvēṟiṭattu naṭakkum. iṉṉa iṭam eṉṟu kaḷam cuṭṭuvatu, tōḻiyiṉ poṟuppākum. ikkāṭcik kaḷaṅkaḷ pakaṟkuṟi, iravukkuṟi eṉappaṭum. talaivaṉōṭu talaiviyiṉ uṟavai aṟintukoṇṭa piṉṉum, tōḻi talaivaṉukku iṭaiyūṟu pōlak kāṭṭik koḷvāḷ. avaṉatu poṅkiya kātalukkuc cevicāyāḷ. avaṉ aritu muyaṉṟu ārvattōṭu koṇṭuvarum kaiyuṟaiyai ēṟka maṟuppāḷ. vēṇṭumeṉṟē kūṭṭam nikaḻāvāṟu nāṭ kaṭattuvāḷ. talaiviyiṉ arumaiyait talaivaṉ uṇara vēṇṭum eṉpatum, talaivaṉiṉ uṟutiyait tāṉ uṇara vēṇṭum eṉpatumē tōḻi iṅṅaṉam ceyvataṉ nōkkam.
akattiṇaikkaṇ maṭal allatu maṭalmā eṉpatu paṉaṅkarukkāl ceyyappaṭṭa kutiraiyaik kuṟikkum. ik kutiraikku āvirampū mālaiyum maṇiyum aṇital uṇṭu. (kuṟun 173) kāmuṟṟa peṇṇai eytappeṟāta āṭavaṉ mārpil elumpu mālaiyum talaiyil erukka mālaiyum koṇṭu maṭalmāmēl ēṟit teruvil varuvāṉ (kuṟun 17, 182). ivvaḻakku maṭalēṟutal eṉappaṭum. itaṉāl tavaiṉiṉ tiṭpattaiyum avaṉ kātaṟku ilakkāṉa naṅkaiyaiyum ūrār aṟivar; mēlum arampōlum paṉaṅkarukkup paṭṭuppaṭṭu avaṉ mēṉiyiliruntu ōṭivarum kurutiyoḻukkaik kaṇṭu irakkaṅ koḷvar. iṉṉa kātaluṉukku makaḷaik koṭukka maṟukkalāmā eṉṟu avaḷ peṟṟōraik kuṟaiyuṅ kūṟuvar. ātaliṉ ūraṟiya maṭalēṟṟam nikaḻātapaṭi maṇaṅkaḷ muṭintuviṭum eṉa aṟiyalām. tōḻi, muṉ colliyapaṭi talaivaṉatu kuṟaikkuc cevicāykkātu, kaiyuṟaikkuk kaikoṭātu kālan tāḻttumpōtu, iṉi maṭalēṟuvatu tāṉ eṇṇiyatu eṇṇiyāṅku eytuvataṟku vaḻi eṉṟu avaṉ avaḷiṭam kūṟuvāṉ.
māveṉa maṭalum ūrpa pūveṉak
kuvimukiḻ erukkaṅ kaṇṇiyum cūṭupa
maṟukiṉ ārkkavum paṭupa
piṟitum ākupa kāmaṅkāḻk koḷiṉē.	(kuṟun. 71)
ikkūṟukai tōḻikkuc ciṟitu kalakkattai uṇṭākkum. ataṉpiṉ tōḻi tuṇaiyāl kaḷavu nīḷavum kāmam ceḻikkavum kāṇkiṉṟōm.
caṅkap pulavar mātaṅkīraṉār pāṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷ iraṇṭē (kuṟun 182, naṟ. 377) avviraṇṭum maṭal māt tuṟai paṟṟiyaṉa. `maṭalpāṭiya mātaṅkīraṉār’ eṉṟa ciṟappup peyar ippulavarkku vaḻaṅkiyiruttalaik karutuṅkāl, iraṇṭu ciṟiya pāṭalkaḷukkāka ip peyar peṟṟirār eṉṟum, ivvoru tuṟaiyil pala pāṭalkaḷ ākkiyiruppar eṉṟum tuṇiyalām. maṭalmāpporuḷ kuṟittup patiṉmūṉṟu pāṭṭukkaḷ caṅka ilakkiyattu uḷa. ivaṟṟuḷ, naṟṟiṇai 143, 152, 342, 377; kuṟuntokai 14, 17, 32, 173, 182 eṉṟa oṉpatum aintiṇaikku uriyavai. kalittokai 138, 139, 140, 141 eṉṟa nāṉkum peruntiṇaikku uriyaṉa. aintiṇai maṭalukkum peruntiṇai maṭalukkum nuṇṇiya periya vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. maṭalmā ēṟuvēṉ eṉṟu vāyaḷaviṟ collutal aintiṇaiyām. “maṭalmā kūṟum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē”(tol. 1047) eṉpatu ilakkaṇam. avvāṟaṉṟi māmēl ēṟiyē kāṭṭum ceykai peruntiṇaiyāy viṭum. “ēṟiya maṭaṟṟiṟam”  (tol. 969) eṉṟa toṭaril `ēṟiya’ eṉavarum iṟanta kālaviṉai peruntiṇai yilakkaṇattaic cuṭṭutal kāṇka.
pakal iravuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷ
